  336:                 || !(oneHour.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(oneHour.getDecision()))
  337:                 || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision())) {
  338:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  339:         }
  340: 
  341:         HardRiskBlock hardBlock = nonAtrHardRiskBlock(current1m);
  342:         if (hardBlock.blocked()) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  343:         if (current1m.getLatestPrice() == null || current1m.getStopLoss() == null || current1m.getTakeProfit() == null
  344:                 || current1m.getStopLoss().signum() <= 0 || current1m.getTakeProfit().signum() <= 0) {
  345:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  346:         }
  347: 
  348:         Evidence evidence = evidence(current1m);
  349:         boolean supportiveCurrent = isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current1m);
  350:         boolean healthyNeutralTiming = current1m.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
  351:                 && (stopLossEvidence != null && stopLossEvidence.hasBoundary() ? evidence.opportunityHealth() : Math.max(evidence.opportunityHealth(), opportunity.getOpportunityHealth())) >= 60
  352:                 && (stopLossEvidence != null && stopLossEvidence.hasBoundary() ? evidence.evidenceScore() : Math.max(evidence.evidenceScore(), opportunity.getEvidenceScore())) >= MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE;
  353:         if (!supportiveCurrent && !healthyNeutralTiming) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  354: 
  355:         if (isBearish(fiveMinuteTrigger.getOriginalDecision())
  356:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
  357:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
  358:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
  359:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()) {
  360:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  361:         }
  362: 
  363:         ExecutionDecision decision = setupTimeframeAtrAuthorityDecision(current1m, evidence, fiveMinuteTrigger);
  364:         EntryQuality quality = assessEntryQuality(current1m, fiveMinuteTrigger.getAtrAtSignal());
  365:         decision = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(decision, quality);
  366:         if (!decision.allowed()) return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(current1m, decision);
  367: 
  368:         String explanation = decision.explanation()
  369:                 + " FIX-014 wake-up: fresh 5m BUY transition " + fiveMinuteTrigger.getId()
  370:                 + " re-evaluated existing opportunity " + opportunity.getId()
  371:                 + " using latest 1m signal " + current1m.getId()
  372:                 + "; 5m remains context/setup authority only and does not execute independently.";
  373:         ExecutionDecision wakeupDecision = ExecutionDecision.allow(
  374:                 decision.source(), "SETUP_TIMEFRAME_WAKEUP", decision.positionPercent(), explanation, decision.evidence());
  375:         saveOpportunity(current1m, evidence, "CONFIRMED", wakeupDecision.source(),
  376:                 wakeupDecision.positionPercent(), wakeupDecision.code(), wakeupDecision.explanation());
  377:         return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(current1m, wakeupDecision);
  378:     }
  379: 
  380:     /**
  381:      * FIX-023 / ETHUSDT 2026-08-20 18:11-18:24 KSA:
  382:      * A fresh 5m BUY transition may wake an already-live, unexecuted opportunity after
  383:      * liquidity/confirmation improves. This is deliberately narrower than granting 5m
  384:      * direct wallet authority: the actual execution price/risk plan remains the latest
  385:      * fresh 1m signal, which must still be supportive, immediately executable, and free
  386:      * of hard risk vetoes. The configured 5m/1h execution profile is revalidated here,
  387:      * so HTF authority is preserved rather than bypassed.
  388:      */
  389:     @Transactional
  390:     public SetupWakeupEvaluation evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeup(
  391:             TradeSignal fiveMinuteTrigger, int currentAllocationPercent) {
  392:         if (stopLossEvidence == null) return evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeupInternal(fiveMinuteTrigger, currentAllocationPercent);
  393:         return stopLossEvidence.wakeup(fiveMinuteTrigger, currentAllocationPercent,
  394:                 () -> evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeupInternal(fiveMinuteTrigger, currentAllocationPercent));
  395:     }
  396: 
  397:     private SetupWakeupEvaluation evaluateConfirmedSetupWakeupInternal(
  398:             TradeSignal fiveMinuteTrigger, int currentAllocationPercent) {
  399:         if (currentAllocationPercent > 0 || fiveMinuteTrigger == null || fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt() == null
  400:                 || !CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL.equals(fiveMinuteTrigger.getInterval())
  401:                 || !isBullish(fiveMinuteTrigger.getDecision())
  402:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isFinalEntryAllowed()
  403:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
  404:                 || !fiveMinuteTrigger.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()) {
  405:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  406:         }
  407: 
  408:         TradeSignal previousFive = previousSignalBefore(
  409:                 fiveMinuteTrigger.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  410:         if (previousFive != null && isBullish(previousFive.getDecision())) {
  411:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  412:         }
  413: 
  414:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity = currentOpportunity(
  415:                 fiveMinuteTrigger.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
  416:                 .filter(o -> o.getExecutedAt() == null)
  417:                 .orElse(null);
  418:         if (opportunity == null) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  419: 
  420:         TradeSignal current1m = latestSignalAtOrBefore(
  421:                 fiveMinuteTrigger.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  422:         if (current1m == null || current1m.getGeneratedAt() == null
  423:                 || Duration.between(current1m.getGeneratedAt(), fiveMinuteTrigger.getGeneratedAt())
  424:                         .compareTo(SETUP_WAKEUP_1M_MAX_AGE) > 0
  425:                 || !isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current1m)
  426:                 || !current1m.isFinalEntryAllowed()
  427:                 || !current1m.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) {
  428:             return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  429:         }
  430: 
  431:         HardRiskBlock hardBlock = nonAtrHardRiskBlock(current1m);
  432:         if (hardBlock.blocked()) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  433: 
  434:         TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult authority =
  435:                 validationService.validateBuyContext(fiveMinuteTrigger, "SETUP_WAKEUP");
  436:         if (!authority.allowed()) return SetupWakeupEvaluation.none();
  437: 
  438:         Evidence evidence = evidence(current1m);
  439:         EntryQuality quality = assessEntryQuality(current1m, fiveMinuteTrigger.getAtrAtSignal());
  440:         int basePercent = Math.min(25, authority.positionPercent());
  441:         ExecutionDecision decision = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(
  442:                 ExecutionDecision.allow(
  443:                         "SETUP_CONFIRMATION_WAKEUP",
  444:                         "FRESH_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  445:                         basePercent,
  446:                         "Fresh 5m BUY transition reactivated an existing unexecuted opportunity after the prior blocker cleared. "
  447:                                 + "Execution still uses latest 1m signal #" + current1m.getId()
  448:                                 + " at " + current1m.getLatestPrice() + "; 5m trigger #" + fiveMinuteTrigger.getId()
  449:                                 + " remains confirmation authority only. HTF profile=" + authority.code() + ".",
  450:                         evidence),
  451:                 quality);
  452:         if (!decision.allowed()) return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(current1m, decision);
  453: 
  454:         saveOpportunity(current1m, evidence, "CONFIRMED", decision.source(),
  455:                 decision.positionPercent(), decision.code(), decision.explanation());
  456:         return new SetupWakeupEvaluation(current1m, decision);
  457:     }
  458: 
  459:     @Transactional
  460:     public ExecutionDecision evaluateBuy(TradeSignal signal, int currentAllocationPercent, String currentStage) {
  461:         if (stopLossEvidence == null) return evaluateBuyInternal(signal, currentAllocationPercent, currentStage);
  462:         return stopLossEvidence.evaluate(signal, currentAllocationPercent,
  463:                 () -> evaluateBuyInternal(signal, currentAllocationPercent, currentStage));
  464:     }
  465: 
  466:     private ExecutionDecision evaluateBuyInternal(TradeSignal signal, int currentAllocationPercent, String currentStage) {
  467:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) {
  468:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("INVALID_SIGNAL", "Signal is missing required execution data.");
  469:         }
  470:         if (!EXECUTION_INTERVAL.equals(signal.getInterval())) {
  471:             return ExecutionDecision.observe("CONTEXT_ONLY", "Only fresh 1m signals may trigger a BUY execution.");
  472:         }
  473: 
  474:         if (isBearish(signal.getDecision())) {
  475:             Evidence evidence = evidence(signal);
  476:             if (isHardBearishReversal(signal, evidence)) {
  477:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "CANCELLED", "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0,
  478:                         "BEARISH_REVERSAL",
  479:                         "BUY opportunity cancelled because bearish evidence is strong or confirmed by 5m/1h context.");
  480:                 return ExecutionDecision.reject("BEARISH_REVERSAL",
  481:                         "Current bearish evidence is strong enough to invalidate the BUY opportunity.", evidence);
  482:             }
  483: 
  484:             if (evidence.opportunityHealth() < OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_MIN_TO_KEEP) {
  485:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "CANCELLED", "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0,
  486:                         "OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_EXHAUSTED",
  487:                         "BUY opportunity health fell below the minimum after bearish/aging penalties.");
  488:                 return ExecutionDecision.reject("OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_EXHAUSTED",
  489:                         "Opportunity memory decayed below the safe minimum and was cancelled.", evidence);
  490:             }
  491: 
  492:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "WEAKENING", "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0,
  493:                     "SOFT_BEARISH_INTERRUPTION",
  494:                     "A brief 1m bearish interruption reduced opportunity evidence instead of erasing it. "
  495:                             + "Health=" + evidence.opportunityHealth() + "/100, evidence=" + evidence.evidenceScore()
  496:                             + ". A new supportive signal is required before execution.");
  497:             return ExecutionDecision.weakening("SOFT_BEARISH_INTERRUPTION",
  498:                     "Bearish 1m evidence weakened the opportunity but did not invalidate supportive 5m/1h context.", evidence);
  499:         }
  500: 
  501:         // Non-ATR safety vetoes remain absolute except for one deliberately tiny,
  502:         // fully-auditable BTC-only exception. An exceptional independent-strength
  503:         // reversal may take a 10-15% probe when BTC is bearish, but only if the
  504:         // asset's own 5m/1h context is no longer bearish and every other hard gate
  505:         // plus ATR immediate-entry permission still passes.
  506:         HardRiskBlock nonAtrHardBlock = nonAtrHardRiskBlock(signal);
  507:         if (nonAtrHardBlock.blocked()) {
  508:             Evidence evidence = evidence(signal);
  509:             ExecutionDecision exceptionalProbe = exceptionalStrengthProbeDecision(signal, evidence, nonAtrHardBlock);
  510:             if (exceptionalProbe != null) {
  511:                 EntryQuality probeQuality = assessEntryQuality(signal);
  512:                 exceptionalProbe = applyExceptionalProbeEntryQualityGuard(exceptionalProbe, probeQuality);
  513:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  514:                         exceptionalProbe.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : exceptionalProbe.state(),
  515:                         exceptionalProbe.source(), exceptionalProbe.positionPercent(),
  516:                         exceptionalProbe.code(), exceptionalProbe.explanation());
  517:                 return exceptionalProbe;
  518:             }
  519:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "BLOCKED", "HARD_RISK", 0,
  520:                     nonAtrHardBlock.code(), nonAtrHardBlock.explanation());
  521:             return ExecutionDecision.reject(nonAtrHardBlock.code(), nonAtrHardBlock.explanation(), evidence);
  522:         }
  523: 
  524:         if (signal.getLatestPrice() == null || signal.getStopLoss() == null || signal.getTakeProfit() == null
  525:                 || signal.getStopLoss().signum() <= 0 || signal.getTakeProfit().signum() <= 0) {
  526:             Evidence evidence = evidence(signal);
  527:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "BLOCKED", "HARD_RISK", 0,
  528:                     "MISSING_RISK_PLAN", "Execution requires a fresh entry price, stop loss, and take-profit plan.");
  529:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("MISSING_RISK_PLAN",
  530:                     "Execution requires a fresh entry price, stop loss, and take-profit plan.", evidence);
  531:         }
  532: 
  533:         // Evaluate deferred continuation BEFORE the ordinary ATR immediate-entry veto.
  534:         // This route exists specifically for a previous analytically valid BUY that was
  535:         // deferred by ATR waiting for a pullback. It still requires a current risk plan,
  536:         // supportive 1m/5m/1h context, healthy opportunity state and >= 1:1 current R/R.
  537:         Evidence evidence = evidence(signal);
  538:         EntryQuality entryQuality = assessEntryQuality(signal);
  539: 
  540:         // Regression safety: a fresh signal that already qualifies for the normal direct-BUY
  541:         // path must not be intercepted by the zero-allocation SCOUT_ENTRY branch. The scout is
  542:         // exploratory authority for supportive signals that are not yet ordinary executable BUYs.
  543:         // Progressive ADD stages remain unchanged because an existing allocation (> 0) still
  544:         // reaches progressivePositionDecision exactly as before.
  545:         //
  546:         // This preserves the documented execution priority:
  547:         // normal executable BUY -> IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION; otherwise consider an initial scout.
  548:         boolean normalDirectBuyCandidate = currentAllocationPercent <= 0 && isDirectBuyCandidate(signal);
  549:         ExecutionDecision progressive = normalDirectBuyCandidate
  550:                 ? null
  551:                 : progressivePositionDecision(
  552:                         signal, evidence, entryQuality, currentAllocationPercent, currentStage);
  553:         if (progressive != null) {
  554:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  555:                     progressive.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : progressive.state(),
  556:                     progressive.source(), progressive.positionPercent(),
  557:                     progressive.code(), progressive.explanation());
  558:             return progressive;
  559:         }
  560: 
  561:         // When a wallet position is already open, only an explicit progressive add
  562:         // may increase exposure. Normal initial-entry routes are not re-run.
  563:         if (currentAllocationPercent > 0) {
  564:             return ExecutionDecision.observe(
  565:                     "POSITION_BUILDING_HOLD",
  566:                     "An open position already exists at " + currentAllocationPercent
  567:                             + "% allocation. No additional stage qualified on this signal.",
  568:                     evidence);
  569:         }
  570: 
  571:         // Breakout retracement handoff runs before the generic reversal retracement route.
  572:         // It remembers a recent BREAKOUT/WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT setup, so the pullback
  573:         // candle does not have to recreate the breakout's volume spike. Production and
  574:         // replay both reach this exact method through the shared evaluateBuy path.
  575:         ExecutionDecision breakoutRetracement = breakoutRetracementDecision(signal, evidence);
  576:         if (breakoutRetracement != null) {
  577:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  578:                     breakoutRetracement.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : breakoutRetracement.state(),
  579:                     breakoutRetracement.source(), breakoutRetracement.positionPercent(),
  580:                     breakoutRetracement.code(), breakoutRetracement.explanation());
  581:             return breakoutRetracement;
  582:         }
  583: 
  584:         // ATR retracement handoff: preserve a recent, high-quality BUY that ATR refused
  585:         // to chase and execute only when price actually returns to ATR's own requested
  586:         // retracement zone. The current 1m candle may be WATCH/NEUTRAL during the pullback;
  587:         // the original thesis is revalidated with as-of 5m context instead of requiring
  588:         // another BUY after price has already started moving again.
  589:         ExecutionDecision retracement = reversalRetracementDecision(signal, evidence);
  590:         if (retracement != null) {
  591:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  592:                     retracement.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : retracement.state(),
  593:                     retracement.source(), retracement.positionPercent(),
  594:                     retracement.code(), retracement.explanation());
  595:             return retracement;
  596:         }
  597: 
  598:         ExecutionDecision deferred = deferredContinuationDecision(signal, evidence);
  599:         if (deferred != null) {
  600:             deferred = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(deferred, entryQuality);
  601:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  602:                     deferred.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : deferred.state(),
  603:                     deferred.source(), deferred.positionPercent(),
  604:                     deferred.code(), deferred.explanation());
  605:             return deferred;
  606:         }
  607: 
  608:         // A fresh 1h BUY plus supportive 5m/1m transition can justify a strictly reduced
  609:         // entry even when ATR is still extended. This is intentionally evaluated before
  610:         // the ordinary 5m-BUY ATR fallback because the 5m may still be WATCH during the
  611:         // first minutes of a genuine higher-timeframe reversal.
  612:         ExecutionDecision transition = higherTimeframeTransitionDecision(signal, evidence);
  613:         if (transition != null) {
  614:             TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(signal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
  615:             EntryQuality transitionQuality = assessEntryQuality(signal, five == null ? signal.getAtrAtSignal() : five.getAtrAtSignal());
  616:             transition = applyTransitionEntryQualityGuard(transition, transitionQuality);
  617:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  618:                     transition.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : transition.state(),
  619:                     transition.source(), transition.positionPercent(),
  620:                     transition.code(), transition.explanation());
  621:             return transition;
  622:         }
  623: 
  624:         // A valid 5m BUY owns setup-level ATR risk. The 1m ATR still controls timing,
  625:         // but it must not permanently kill a fresh 5m setup whose own ATR explicitly
  626:         // permits immediate/reduced entry. If a fresh 1h context exists and is bearish,
  627:         // it still vetoes this route. Missing 1h context does not manufacture a veto.
  628:         TradeSignal setupAtrSignal = setupTimeframeAtrAuthoritySignal(signal, evidence);
  629:         if (setupAtrSignal != null) {
  630:             ExecutionDecision setupAtrAuthority = setupTimeframeAtrAuthorityDecision(signal, evidence, setupAtrSignal);
  631:             // Entry-quality ATR extension must use the same setup timeframe that owns
  632:             // ATR risk. Using the much smaller 1m ATR here can falsely label a valid
  633:             // 5m setup as a 9-10 ATR chase even when the 5m risk plan permits entry.
  634:             EntryQuality setupEntryQuality = assessEntryQuality(signal, setupAtrSignal.getAtrAtSignal());
  635:             setupAtrAuthority = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(setupAtrAuthority, setupEntryQuality);
  636:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  637:                     setupAtrAuthority.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : setupAtrAuthority.state(),
  638:                     setupAtrAuthority.source(), setupAtrAuthority.positionPercent(),
  639:                     setupAtrAuthority.code(), setupAtrAuthority.explanation());
  640:             return setupAtrAuthority;
  641:         }
  642: 
  643:         // For every remaining non-continuation path, ATR immediate-entry permission remains mandatory.
  644:         if (!signal.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) {
  645:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, "BLOCKED", "HARD_RISK", 0,
  646:                     "ATR_ENTRY_BLOCKED", "ATR risk controls do not allow immediate entry.");
  647:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("ATR_ENTRY_BLOCKED",
  648:                     "ATR risk controls do not allow immediate entry.", evidence);
  649:         }
  650: 
  651:         // Fast path: a normal fresh BUY can execute immediately using the configured profile.
  652:         if (isDirectBuyCandidate(signal)) {
  653:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation = validationService.validateBuy(signal, entryQuality.score());
  654:             if (validation.allowed()) {
  655:                 ExecutionDecision postBearishGuard = balancedEarlyPostBearishGuard(signal, evidence, entryQuality, validation);
  656:                 if (postBearishGuard != null) {
  657:                     saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  658:                             postBearishGuard.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : postBearishGuard.state(),
  659:                             postBearishGuard.source(), postBearishGuard.positionPercent(),
  660:                             postBearishGuard.code(), postBearishGuard.explanation());
  661:                     return postBearishGuard;
  662:                 }
  663: 
  664:                 // FIX-041 / ACEUSDT 2026-08-22 13:28 KSA:
  665:                 // BALANCED_EARLY is an early-entry profile by definition. If the independent
  666:                 // Entry Quality model already classifies the price as LATE_ENTRY, executing
  667:                 // this route would contradict its purpose and can turn a well-detected trend
  668:                 // into a chase near exhaustion. Keep every other proven initial-entry route
  669:                 // unchanged: setup-timeframe ATR authority, confirmation wake-up, accumulated
  670:                 // evidence, recovery probes and normal non-BALANCED_EARLY BUYs still use the
  671:                 // existing quality guard. Production and Replay share this exact method.
  672:                 if ("BALANCED_EARLY".equals(validation.code())
  673:                         && "LATE_ENTRY".equals(entryQuality.classification())) {
  674:                     ExecutionDecision lateBalancedEarly = ExecutionDecision.building(
  675:                             "BALANCED_EARLY_LATE_ENTRY_BLOCKED",
  676:                             "BALANCED_EARLY requires an actually early price. Entry Quality is "
  677:                                     + entryQuality.score() + "/100 (" + entryQuality.classification()
  678:                                     + "), so the opportunity stays alive for a better price or fresh "
  679:                                     + "confirmation instead of opening a new late-stage position.",
  680:                             evidence);
  681:                     saveOpportunity(signal, evidence, lateBalancedEarly.state(),
  682:                             "OPPORTUNITY_MEMORY", 0, lateBalancedEarly.code(), lateBalancedEarly.explanation());
  683:                     return lateBalancedEarly;
  684:                 }
  685: 
  686:                 ExecutionDecision guarded = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(
  687:                         ExecutionDecision.allow(
  688:                                 "IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION",
  689:                                 validation.code(),
  690:                                 validation.positionPercent(),
  691:                                 validation.explanation(),
  692:                                 evidence
  693:                         ),
  694:                         entryQuality);
  695:                 // FIX-11G diagnostics: validation can grant the transitional 25% authority,
  696:                 // but the shared downstream Entry Quality/authority guard remains final. Log
  697:                 // the post-guard result so Production and Replay investigations can distinguish
  698:                 // "FIX-11G qualified" from "FIX-11G actually remained executable".
  699:                 if ("BALANCED_NEUTRAL_5M_WATCH_1H".equals(validation.code())) {
  700:                     log.info("FIX-11G post-guard result: signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, allowed={}, "
  701:                                     + "state={}, source={}, code={}, positionPercent={}, entryQuality={}",
  702:                             signal.getId(), signal.getSymbol(), signal.getGeneratedAt(), guarded.allowed(),
  703:                             guarded.state(), guarded.source(), guarded.code(), guarded.positionPercent(),
  704:                             entryQuality.score());
  705:                 }
  706:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  707:                         guarded.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : guarded.state(),
  708:                         guarded.source(), guarded.positionPercent(),
  709:                         guarded.code(), guarded.explanation());
  710:                 return guarded;
  711:             }
  712: 
  713:             // Preserve the existing BUY-only persistence model as the first consolidation route.
  714:             OpportunityConsolidationService.Assessment consolidated = consolidationService.evaluate(signal);
  715:             if (consolidated.allowed()) {
  716:                 ExecutionDecision guarded = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(
  717:                         ExecutionDecision.allow(
  718:                                 "CONSOLIDATED_BUY",
  719:                                 consolidated.code(),
  720:                                 consolidated.positionPercent(),
  721:                                 consolidated.explanation(),
  722:                                 evidence
  723:                         ),
  724:                         entryQuality);
  725:                 saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  726:                         guarded.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : guarded.state(),
  727:                         guarded.source(), guarded.positionPercent(),
  728:                         guarded.code(), guarded.explanation());
  729:                 return guarded;
  730:             }
  731:         }
  732: 
  733:         // EARLY-ENTRY PATH (separate from the normal BUY path).
  734:         // It is reached only after every existing normal/special initial-entry route above
  735:         // had the opportunity to act. A valid normal BUY therefore always keeps priority.
  736:         // The pressure service reads closed candles as-of this signal timestamp; replay calls
  737:         // this exact production method and therefore exercises the exact same logic.
  738:         ExecutionDecision pressureProbe = pressureProbeDecision(signal, evidence);
  739:         if (pressureProbe != null) {
  740:             pressureProbe = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(pressureProbe, entryQuality);
  741:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  742:                     pressureProbe.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : pressureProbe.state(),
  743:                     pressureProbe.source(), pressureProbe.positionPercent(),
  744:                     pressureProbe.code(), pressureProbe.explanation());
  745:             return pressureProbe;
  746:         }
  747: 
  748:         // FIX-026 / ENAUSDT 2026-08-20 12:55-13:04 KSA:
  749:         // The market can transition from a recently bearish 1m state into absorption and
  750:         // then a confirmed recovery before the ordinary strategy label leaves RANGE. The
  751:         // historical ENA regression moved from STRONG_SELL 15 -> WATCH 75 while three
  752:         // CLOSED candles printed 85.26%, 87.47% and 73.91% taker-buy pressure and rising
  753:         // closes. Waiting for a later conventional BUY gave away the early edge. This route
  754:         // does NOT lower BUY thresholds or rewrite RANGE: it allows only a 25% recovery probe
  755:         // after (a) a recent bearish state, (b) a closed-candle absorption/recovery sequence,
  756:         // (c) strong current technical recovery, and (d) all existing ATR/context hard gates.
  757:         // RecoveryTransitionService selects candles by close_time <= signal.generated_at, so
  758:         // Production and Proven/Replay cannot inspect the still-open or future candle.
  759:         ExecutionDecision recoveryTransition = recoveryTransitionDecision(signal, evidence);
  760:         if (recoveryTransition != null) {
  761:             recoveryTransition = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(recoveryTransition, entryQuality);
  762:             saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  763:                     recoveryTransition.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : recoveryTransition.state(),
  764:                     recoveryTransition.source(), recoveryTransition.positionPercent(),
  765:                     recoveryTransition.code(), recoveryTransition.explanation());
  766:             return recoveryTransition;
  767:         }
  768: 
  769:         // Intelligent evidence path: BUY and strong WATCH observations can build one opportunity.
  770:         // FIX-119: accumulated evidence may prove that an opportunity is still valid without
  771:         // proving that the current price is a good place to open the initial position. Keep
  772:         // the existing shared Entry Quality guard first, then defer only LATE_ENTRY
  773:         // ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE decisions that have neither accumulated BUY evidence nor a
  774:         // fresh bullish 5m setup. The opportunity remains BUILDING and is re-evaluated by
  775:         // the existing lifecycle on subsequent signals. All other execution routes are
  776:         // structurally outside this guard.
  777:         ExecutionDecision accumulated = applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(
  778:                 accumulatedDecision(signal, evidence), entryQuality);
  779:         accumulated = applyAccumulatedLateEntryTimingGuard(
  780:                 signal, accumulated, evidence, entryQuality);
  781:         saveOpportunity(signal, evidence,
  782:                 accumulated.allowed() ? "CONFIRMED" : accumulated.state(),
  783:                 accumulated.source(), accumulated.positionPercent(),
  784:                 accumulated.code(), accumulated.explanation());
  785:         return accumulated;
  786:     }
  787: 
  788:     @Transactional
  789:     public void markExecuted(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision decision) {
  790:         if (signal == null || decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return;
  791:         currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
  792:                 .ifPresent(opportunity -> {
  793:                     boolean stillBuilding = "SCOUT_ENTRY".equals(decision.source())
  794:                             || "PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY".equals(decision.source())
  795:                             || "CONFIRMATION_ADD".equals(decision.source());
  796:                     opportunity.setStatus(stillBuilding ? "BUILDING" : "EXECUTED");
  797:                     opportunity.setExecutionSource(decision.source());
  798:                     opportunity.setRecommendedPositionPercent(decision.positionPercent());
  799:                     opportunity.setDecisionCode(decision.code());
  800:                     opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(decision.explanation());
  801:                     opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
  802:                     opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
  803:                     if (!stillBuilding) {
  804:                         opportunity.setExecutedAt(Instant.now());
  805:                     }
  806:                     opportunity.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
  807:                     saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
  808:                 });
  809:     }
  810: 
  811: 
  812:     /**
  813:      * FIX-020 / ENAUSDT 2026-08-20:
  814:      * A fully closed position is an immutable evidence boundary. Scout/probe/confirmation
  815:      * opportunities intentionally remain BUILDING while the SAME position is open, but once
  816:      * that position reaches any terminal exit (TP, SL, SIGNAL_SELL, profit lock, manual close),
  817:      * its pre-exit evidence may never finance a brand-new position. Replay calls this exact
  818:      * method too, so production and Proven/Regression share the same lifecycle semantics.
  819:      */
  820:     @Transactional
  821:     public void completePositionOpportunity(String symbol, TradeSignal exitSignal, String closeReason) {
  822:         if (symbol == null || symbol.isBlank()) return;
  823:         currentOpportunity(symbol, List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
  824:                 .ifPresent(opportunity -> {
  825:                     opportunity.setStatus("COMPLETED");
  826:                     if (exitSignal != null) {
  827:                         opportunity.setLatestSignal(exitSignal);
  828:                         if (exitSignal.getGeneratedAt() != null) {
  829:                             opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(exitSignal.getGeneratedAt());
  830:                         }
  831:                     }
  832:                     opportunity.setDecisionCode("POSITION_CLOSED_EVIDENCE_BOUNDARY");
  833:                     opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(
  834:                             "Position lifecycle completed"
  835:                                     + (closeReason == null || closeReason.isBlank() ? "" : " via " + closeReason)
  836:                                     + "; pre-exit opportunity evidence is consumed and cannot justify a new position.");
  837:                     opportunity.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
  838:                     saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
  839:                 });
  840:     }
  841: 
  842: 
  843: 
  844:     /**
  845:      * Entry quality is deliberately independent from signal quality. A market can become
  846:      * more certain while simultaneously becoming a worse price to enter.
  847:      */
  848:     public EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current) {
  849:         return assessEntryQuality(current, current == null ? null : current.getAtrAtSignal(),
  850:                 current == null ? null : current.getLatestPrice());
  851:     }
  852: 
  853:     /**
  854:      * FIX-056: re-score entry quality against the actual execution-time market price.
  855:      * The immutable signal price remains decision evidence only.
  856:      */
  857:     public EntryQuality assessEntryQualityAtPrice(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
  858:         return assessEntryQuality(current, current == null ? null : current.getAtrAtSignal(), executionPrice);
  859:     }
  860: 
  861:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr) {
  862:         return assessEntryQuality(current, authoritativeAtr, current == null ? null : current.getLatestPrice());
  863:     }
  864: 
  865:     private EntryQuality assessEntryQuality(TradeSignal current, BigDecimal authoritativeAtr, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
  866:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || executionPrice == null
  867:                 || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
  868:             return new EntryQuality(0, "UNKNOWN", 0d, 0d, 0d, 0L, false, null, 0, 0d, false, null);
  869:         }
  870: 
  871:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
  872:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
  873: 
  874:         BigDecimal reference = executionPrice;
  875:         BigDecimal recentHigh = executionPrice;
  876:         Instant recentHighAt = current.getGeneratedAt();
  877:         for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
  878:             if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
  879:             if (s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
  880:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
  881:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  882:                 reference = s.getLatestPrice();
  883:             }
  884:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7: mirror the existing rolling-low memory with a rolling high.
  885:             // We intentionally use already-persisted signal prices so Replay and Production
  886:             // observe the same pre-wallet evidence and no new market-data dependency is added.
  887:             if (s.getLatestPrice() != null && s.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0
  888:                     && s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) > 0) {
  889:                 recentHigh = s.getLatestPrice();
  890:                 recentHighAt = s.getGeneratedAt();
  891:             }
  892:         }
  893: 
  894:         // FIX-055 + FIX-105: keep the opportunity anchor/best floor only while the opportunity
  895:         // is fresh. currentOpportunity() is Replay-aware, and age is measured exclusively from
  896:         // persisted/replayed candle-time evidence (startedAt -> signal.generatedAt), never wall-clock
  897:         // time. Therefore Production and Replay execute this exact same branch deterministically.
  898:         java.util.Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> activeOpportunity = currentOpportunity(
  899:                 current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"));
  900:         String chaseReferenceSource = "ROLLING_30M";
  901:         if (activeOpportunity.isPresent()) {
  902:             ExecutionOpportunity opp = activeOpportunity.get();
  903:             boolean anchorStillFresh = false;
  904:             long opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = 0L;
  905: 
  906:             if (opp.getStartedAt() != null) {
  907:                 Duration opportunityAge = Duration.between(opp.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt());
  908:                 opportunityAgeMinutesForReference = Math.max(0L, opportunityAge.toMinutes());
  909:                 // FIX-105 boundary is inclusive: exactly 120 minutes keeps FIX-055 protection;
  910:                 // the first instant beyond two hours returns authority to the rolling 30m reference.
  911:                 anchorStillFresh = !opportunityAge.isNegative()
  912:                         && opportunityAge.compareTo(ANCHOR_MAX_AGE) <= 0;
  913:             }
  914: 
  915:             if (anchorStillFresh) {
  916:                 BigDecimal anchor = opp.getAnchorEntryPrice();
  917:                 BigDecimal best = opp.getBestEntryPrice();
  918:                 if (anchor != null && anchor.signum() > 0 && anchor.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  919:                     reference = anchor;
  920:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_ANCHOR";
  921:                 }
  922:                 if (best != null && best.signum() > 0 && best.compareTo(reference) < 0) {
  923:                     reference = best;
  924:                     chaseReferenceSource = "OPPORTUNITY_BEST";
  925:                 }
  926:             }
  927: 
  928:             // FIX-105 diagnostics are deliberately log-only in v1: no schema expansion is required
  929:             // to prove which reference won in Production or Replay. Persisted decision/opportunity
  930:             // evidence remains unchanged.
  931:             log.debug("FIX-105 chase reference symbol={} source={} reference={} opportunityAgeMinutes={} anchorFresh={}",
  932:                     current.getSymbol(), chaseReferenceSource, reference, opportunityAgeMinutesForReference, anchorStillFresh);
  933:         }
  934: 
  935:         double expansionPercent = executionPrice.subtract(reference)
  936:                 .divide(reference, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  937:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
  938: 
  939:         double atrExtension = 0d;
  940:         BigDecimal atrForExtension = authoritativeAtr != null && authoritativeAtr.signum() > 0
  941:                 ? authoritativeAtr
  942:                 : current.getAtrAtSignal();
  943:         if (atrForExtension != null && atrForExtension.signum() > 0) {
  944:             atrExtension = executionPrice.subtract(reference).max(BigDecimal.ZERO)
  945:                     .divide(atrForExtension, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
  946:         }
  947: 
  948:         long ageMinutes = activeOpportunity
  949:                 .map(o -> o.getStartedAt() == null ? 0L
  950:                         : Math.max(0L, Duration.between(o.getStartedAt(), current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes()))
  951:                 .orElse(0L);
  952: 
  953:         double rr = currentRewardRisk(current, executionPrice);
  954: 
  955:         // FIX-091 / Fix 7: a prior high is considered a recent rejection only when price
  956:         // subsequently backed away and the current entry is returning close to that same zone.
  957:         // v1 is deliberately conservative: this only reduces Entry Quality; it never hard-vetoes.
  958:         int rejectionCount = 0;
  959:         double distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = 0d;
  960:         BigDecimal recentRejectedHigh = null;
  961:         if (recentHigh != null && recentHigh.signum() > 0 && recentHighAt != null
  962:                 && recentHighAt.isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())) {
  963:             for (TradeSignal s : recent) {
  964:                 if (s.getGeneratedAt() == null || !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(recentHighAt)
  965:                         || s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()) || s.getLatestPrice() == null) continue;
  966:                 if (s.getLatestPrice().compareTo(recentHigh) < 0) rejectionCount++;
  967:             }
  968:             distanceFromRejectedHighPercent = recentHigh.subtract(executionPrice).abs()
  969:                     .divide(recentHigh, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
  970:                     .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).doubleValue();
  971:             if (rejectionCount >= 2 && distanceFromRejectedHighPercent <= 0.50d) {
  972:                 recentRejectedHigh = recentHigh;
  973:             }
  974:         }
  975: 
  976:         int score = 100;
  977: 
  978:         if (expansionPercent > 8d) score -= 50;
  979:         else if (expansionPercent > 5d) score -= 35;
  980:         else if (expansionPercent > 3d) score -= 22;
  981:         else if (expansionPercent > 1.5d) score -= 10;
  982: 
  983:         if (atrExtension > 6d) score -= 30;
  984:         else if (atrExtension > 4d) score -= 20;
  985:         else if (atrExtension > 2.5d) score -= 10;
  986: 
  987:         if (ageMinutes > 60) score -= 15;
  988:         else if (ageMinutes > 40) score -= 10;
  989:         else if (ageMinutes > 20) score -= 5;
  990: 
  991:         if (rr <= 0d || rr < 1d) score -= 30;
  992:         else if (rr < 1.25d) score -= 15;
  993:         else if (rr >= 2d) score += 5;
  994: 
  995:         if (recentRejectedHigh != null) {
  996:             // FIX-091 / Fix 7 v1 is penalty/size-reduction only. A rejection penalty must not
  997:             // by itself push an otherwise non-chase entry below the hard CHASE_ENTRY cutoff.
  998:             int beforeRejectionPenalty = score;
  999:             score -= RECENT_REJECTION_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
 1000:             if (beforeRejectionPenalty >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
 1001:                 score = Math.max(CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF, score);
 1002:             }
 1003:         }
 1004: 
 1005:         if ("HIGH_VOLATILITY".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))) score -= 10;
 1006: 
 1007:         // FIX-055: STOP_EXPOSED remains a soft warning (TARGET_BLOCKED is still the
 1008:         // hard liquidity veto), but an exposed stop must reduce entry quality and
 1009:         // therefore position size or block a chase through the existing quality guard.
 1010:         if (current.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.STOP_EXPOSED) {
 1011:             score -= STOP_EXPOSED_ENTRY_QUALITY_PENALTY;
 1012:         }
 1013: 
 1014:         if ("BREAKOUT".equals(String.valueOf(current.getMarketRegime()))
 1015:                 && expansionPercent <= 3d && atrExtension <= 2.5d) score += 5;
 1016: 
 1017:         score = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, score));
 1018:         String classification = score >= 85 ? "EXCELLENT_ENTRY"
 1019:                 : score >= 70 ? "GOOD_ENTRY"
 1020:                 : score >= 55 ? "ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY"
 1021:                 : score >= CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF ? "LATE_ENTRY"
 1022:                 : "CHASE_ENTRY";
 1023: 
 1024:         boolean moderateBtcConflict = current.getBtcContextStatus() == com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.CONFLICT;
 1025:         boolean transitionProbe = "TRANSITION_PROBE".equals(current.getEntryAuthority());
 1026:         Integer authorityCap = current.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent();
 1027:         return new EntryQuality(score, classification, expansionPercent, atrExtension, rr, ageMinutes,
 1028:                 moderateBtcConflict, recentRejectedHigh, rejectionCount, distanceFromRejectedHighPercent,
 1029:                 transitionProbe, authorityCap);
 1030:     }
 1031: 
 1032:     private ExecutionDecision progressivePositionDecision(
 1033:             TradeSignal current,
 1034:             Evidence e,
 1035:             EntryQuality q,
 1036:             int currentAllocationPercent,
 1037:             String currentStage) {
 1038: 
 1039:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1040:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1041:         boolean fiveSupportive = five != null
 1042:                 && (five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getDecision()))
 1043:                 && five.getTotalScore() >= 60 && five.getConfidenceScore() >= 65;
 1044:         boolean oneHourSupportive = oneHour != null
 1045:                 && (oneHour.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(oneHour.getDecision()));
 1046: 
 1047:         if (currentAllocationPercent <= 0) {
 1048:             ImmediatePriceAction priceAction = immediatePriceAction(current);
 1049:             boolean scout = isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)
 1050:                     && priceAction.supportive()
 1051:                     && current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1052:                     && current.getTotalScore() >= SCOUT_MIN_SIGNAL_SCORE
 1053:                     && current.getConfidenceScore() >= SCOUT_MIN_CONFIDENCE
 1054:                     && q.score() >= SCOUT_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
 1055:                     && q.rewardRisk() >= 1.25d
 1056:                     && e.opportunityHealth() >= 55
 1057:                     && e.evidenceMomentum() >= -5
 1058:                     && fiveSupportive
 1059:                     && oneHourSupportive;
 1060:             if (scout) {
 1061:                 return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1062:                         "SCOUT_ENTRY",
 1063:                         "EXCELLENT_PRICE_SCOUT",
 1064:                         SCOUT_TARGET_PERCENT,
 1065:                         "Progressive Position Building opened a " + SCOUT_TARGET_PERCENT
 1066:                                 + "% scout because signal quality is supportive while entry quality is "
 1067:                                 + q.score() + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "). Price expansion="
 1068:                                 + format(q.expansionPercent()) + "%, ATR extension=" + format(q.atrExtension())
 1069:                                 + ", current R/R=" + format(q.rewardRisk())
 1070:                                 + ". Immediate closed-candle price action=" + priceAction.summary()
 1071:                                 + ". Capital remains deliberately small until confirmation improves.",
 1072:                         e);
 1073:             }
 1074:             return null;
 1075:         }
 1076: 
 1077:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current) || !fiveSupportive || !oneHourSupportive
 1078:                 || q.classification().equals("CHASE_ENTRY")) {
 1079:             // FIX-154: the existing raw 1h guard remains the authority for add-ons.
 1080:             // Only after it has rejected an add do we write optional audit evidence;
 1081:             // this call can never change the decision and is skipped on successful paths.
 1082:             if (!oneHourSupportive) {
 1083:                 validationService.recordProgressiveAssessmentIfSoftBlocked(current);
 1084:             }
 1085:             return null;
 1086:         }
 1087: 
 1088:         if (currentAllocationPercent < CONFIRMATION_TARGET_PERCENT) {
 1089:             boolean evidenceConfirmed = e.evidenceScore() >= MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE
 1090:                     && e.averageScore() >= 65
 1091:                     && e.averageConfidence() >= 65;
 1092:             boolean directConfirmed = isBullish(current.getDecision())
 1093:                     && current.getTotalScore() >= properties.minimumBuyScore();
 1094:             if ((evidenceConfirmed || directConfirmed)
 1095:                     && current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1096:                     && e.opportunityHealth() >= 60
 1097:                     && q.score() >= CONFIRMATION_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
 1098:                     && q.rewardRisk() >= 1.15d) {
 1099:                 int add = CONFIRMATION_TARGET_PERCENT - currentAllocationPercent;
 1100:                 return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1101:                         "CONFIRMATION_ADD",
 1102:                         "PROGRESSIVE_CONFIRMATION_ADD",
 1103:                         add,
 1104:                         "Progressive Position Building added " + add
 1105:                                 + "% after the scout was confirmed. Entry quality=" + q.score()
 1106:                                 + "/100, evidence=" + e.evidenceScore()
 1107:                                 + ", health=" + e.opportunityHealth()
 1108:                                 + ", 5m=" + e.fiveMinute() + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + ".",
 1109:                         e);
 1110:             }
 1111:         }
 1112: 
 1113:         if (currentAllocationPercent >= CONFIRMATION_TARGET_PERCENT
 1114:                 && currentAllocationPercent < TREND_TARGET_PERCENT) {
 1115:             boolean strongCurrent = isBullish(current.getDecision())
 1116:                     || (current.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH
 1117:                     && current.getTotalScore() >= 72
 1118:                     && current.getConfidenceScore() >= 75);
 1119:             boolean strongFive = five != null
 1120:                     && (isBullish(five.getDecision()) || five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH)
 1121:                     && five.getTotalScore() >= 65
 1122:                     && five.getConfidenceScore() >= 70;
 1123:             if (strongCurrent && strongFive
 1124:                     && current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1125:                     && e.opportunityHealth() >= 70
 1126:                     && e.evidenceMomentum() >= 0
 1127:                     && q.score() >= TREND_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
 1128:                     && q.rewardRisk() >= 1.20d) {
 1129:                 int add = TREND_TARGET_PERCENT - currentAllocationPercent;
 1130:                 return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1131:                         "TREND_ADD",
 1132:                         "PROGRESSIVE_TREND_ADD",
 1133:                         add,
 1134:                         "Progressive Position Building completed the position with a " + add
 1135:                                 + "% trend add only after confirmation strengthened. Entry quality="
 1136:                                 + q.score() + "/100, health=" + e.opportunityHealth()
 1137:                                 + ", evidence momentum=" + e.evidenceMomentum()
 1138:                                 + ", current R/R=" + format(q.rewardRisk()) + ".",
 1139:                         e);
 1140:             }
 1141:         }
 1142:         return null;
 1143:     }
 1144: 
 1145:     /**
 1146:      * FIX-056: final execution-time revalidation. The signal decision remains immutable,
 1147:      * but a wallet BUY must be re-checked against the current canonical market price before
 1148:      * sizing/fill. This prevents a valid old signal from becoming a late chase by execution time.
 1149:      */
 1150:     public ExecutionDecision revalidateAtExecutionPrice(TradeSignal signal, ExecutionDecision approvedDecision, BigDecimal executionPrice) {
 1151:         // FIX-122: recheck only stamped initial approvals; ADD approvals carry no stamp.
 1152:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) approvedDecision = stopLossEvidence.recheck(signal, approvedDecision);
 1153:         if (approvedDecision != null && !approvedDecision.allowed()) return approvedDecision;
 1154:         if (signal == null || approvedDecision == null || !approvedDecision.allowed()
 1155:                 || executionPrice == null || executionPrice.signum() <= 0) {
 1156:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_INVALID",
 1157:                     "A fresh positive execution price is required before wallet execution.");
 1158:         }
 1159:         if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && executionPrice.compareTo(signal.getStopLoss()) <= 0) {
 1160:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_AT_OR_BELOW_STOP",
 1161:                     "Current execution price is already at/below the signal stop; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());
 1162:         }
 1163:         if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && executionPrice.compareTo(signal.getTakeProfit()) >= 0) {
 1164:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("EXECUTION_PRICE_AT_OR_ABOVE_TARGET",
 1165:                     "Current execution price has already reached/exceeded the signal target; BUY cancelled.", approvedDecision.evidence());
 1166:         }
 1167:         EntryQuality quality = assessEntryQualityAtPrice(signal, executionPrice);
 1168:         return applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(approvedDecision, quality).withStopBoundary(approvedDecision.stopBoundary());
 1169:     }
 1170: 
 1171:     private ExecutionDecision applyInitialEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
 1172:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
 1173:         if (q.score() < CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF) {
 1174:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1175:                     "CHASE_ENTRY_BLOCKED",
 1176:                     "The market signal is valid, but Entry Quality is only " + q.score()
 1177:                             + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "). Price has already expanded "
 1178:                             + format(q.expansionPercent()) + "% from the recent opportunity base and "
 1179:                             + format(q.atrExtension()) + " ATR. The engine will not buy a late-stage chase.",
 1180:                     decision.evidence());
 1181:         }
 1182: 
 1183:         int cap = q.score() >= 85 ? 50 : q.score() >= 70 ? 40 : 25;
 1184:         StringBuilder capReason = new StringBuilder(" Entry Quality ")
 1185:                 .append(q.score()).append("/100 (").append(q.classification()).append(")");
 1186: 
 1187:         // FIX-091 / Fix 1: moderate BTC conflict stays fast and BUY-eligible, but may not
 1188:         // receive full exposure. Classification remains owned by BtcMarketContextService.
 1189:         if (q.moderateBtcConflict()) {
 1190:             int btcConflictCap = q.score() >= 85 ? BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_EXCELLENT_CAP
 1191:                     : q.score() >= 70 ? BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_GOOD_CAP
 1192:                     : BTC_MODERATE_CONFLICT_LATE_CAP;
 1193:             cap = Math.min(cap, btcConflictCap);
 1194:             capReason.append("; BTC context is CONFLICT, so the same tiered initial-entry model reduces exposure to ")
 1195:                     .append(btcConflictCap).append("%");
 1196:         }
 1197:         // FIX-091 / Fix 5: EntryAuthority is only a maximum size. The current signal has already
 1198:         // travelled through the normal direct-candidate + validateBuy flow before this guard runs.
 1199:         // The EntryQuality snapshot is created from the current TradeSignal, so the explicit cap is
 1200:         // carried through the signal fields without inventing a parallel execution route.
 1201:         if (q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent() != null && q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent() > 0) {
 1202:             cap = Math.min(cap, q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
 1203:             if (q.transitionProbe()) {
 1204:                 capReason.append("; BREAKOUT_TRANSITION probe authority caps exposure at ")
 1205:                         .append(q.entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent()).append("%");
 1206:             }
 1207:         }
 1208:         if (q.recentRejectedHigh() != null) {
 1209:             capReason.append("; recent rejection zone near ").append(q.recentRejectedHigh())
 1210:                     .append(" reduced entry quality without creating a hard veto");
 1211:         }
 1212: 
 1213:         int reduced = Math.min(decision.positionPercent(), cap);
 1214:         if (reduced == decision.positionPercent()) return decision;
 1215: 
 1216:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1217:                 decision.source(),
 1218:                 decision.code(),
 1219:                 reduced,
 1220:                 decision.explanation() + capReason + " capped the initial allocation at " + reduced
 1221:                         + "% so confirmation can add later instead of committing full size at once.",
 1222:                 decision.evidence());
 1223:     }
 1224: 
 1225:     /**
 1226:      * FIX-119 ƒ?" Accumulated-evidence late-entry timing deferral.
 1227:      *
 1228:      * ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE is opportunity memory. It may legitimately open a
 1229:      * position without a current BUY when persistent WATCH evidence has matured;
 1230:      * ICPUSDT #1702 is a proven example and must remain executable.
 1231:      *
 1232:      * The defect appears when that same WATCH-only authority reaches a price that
 1233:      * Entry Quality already classifies as LATE_ENTRY. In the current implementation,
 1234:      * scores 50-54 remain executable with reduced sizing, including cases where a
 1235:      * recent rejection-zone penalty was deliberately floored at the 50-point chase
 1236:      * cutoff. Historical BTCUSDT #1701, EDUUSDT #1716 and UNIUSDT #1731 demonstrate
 1237:      * that this can convert a valid opportunity into a poorly timed initial entry.
 1238:      *
 1239:      * FIX-119 therefore defers only when all of the following are simultaneously true:
 1240:      *   - the candidate is an allowed ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE initial entry;
 1241:      *   - Entry Quality is LATE_ENTRY;
 1242:      *   - accumulated fresh 1m evidence contains zero BUY observations; and
 1243:      *   - fresh 5m setup context is not BUY/STRONG_BUY.
 1244:      *
 1245:      * The result stays BUILDING rather than being rejected. Existing opportunity
 1246:      * evidence is preserved by saveOpportunity(), so subsequent signals can release
 1247:      * the opportunity when price geometry improves or fresh BUY authority appears.
 1248:      *
 1249:      * Good controls intentionally preserved:
 1250:      *   - ICPUSDT #1702: 0 BUY / 10 WATCH but ACCEPTABLE_ENTRY 55 -> unchanged.
 1251:      *   - SUIUSDT #1696: LATE_ENTRY 50 but accumulated BUY evidence and 5m BUY
 1252:      *     -> unchanged.
 1253:      *
 1254:      * A 1h BUY by itself does not bypass this guard. EDUUSDT #1716 had 1h BUY /
 1255:      * BALANCED_STRONG but remained a badly timed WATCH-only accumulated entry.
 1256:      */
 1257:     private ExecutionDecision applyAccumulatedLateEntryTimingGuard(
 1258:             TradeSignal signal,
 1259:             ExecutionDecision decision,
 1260:             Evidence evidence,
 1261:             EntryQuality entryQuality) {
 1262: 
 1263:         if (decision == null
 1264:                 || !decision.allowed()
 1265:                 || !"ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE".equals(decision.source())
 1266:                 || evidence == null
 1267:                 || entryQuality == null) {
 1268:             return decision;
 1269:         }
 1270: 
 1271:         if (!"LATE_ENTRY".equals(entryQuality.classification())) {
 1272:             return decision;
 1273:         }
 1274: 
 1275:         boolean hasAccumulatedBuyEvidence = evidence.buyCount() > 0;
 1276:         boolean hasFreshFiveMinuteBuy = isBullish(evidence.fiveMinute());
 1277: 
 1278:         if (hasAccumulatedBuyEvidence || hasFreshFiveMinuteBuy) {
 1279:             return decision;
 1280:         }
 1281: 
 1282:         String rejectionContext = entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh() == null
 1283:                 ? "none"
 1284:                 : entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh()
 1285:                         + " (distance="
 1286:                         + format(entryQuality.distanceFromRejectedHighPercent())
 1287:                         + "%)";
 1288: 
 1289:         log.info(
 1290:                 "[FIX-119][ACCUMULATED_LATE_ENTRY_DEFERRED] "
 1291:                         + "signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, "
 1292:                         + "entryQuality={}/{}, rejectionZone={}, "
 1293:                         + "buyCount={}, watchCount={}, neutralCount={}, "
 1294:                         + "evidenceScore={}, averageScore={}, averageConfidence={}, "
 1295:                         + "fiveMinute={}, oneHour={}, result=BUILDING",
 1296:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getId(),
 1297:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getSymbol(),
 1298:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 1299:                 signal == null ? null : signal.getLatestPrice(),
 1300:                 entryQuality.score(),
 1301:                 entryQuality.classification(),
 1302:                 rejectionContext,
 1303:                 evidence.buyCount(),
 1304:                 evidence.watchCount(),
 1305:                 evidence.neutralCount(),
 1306:                 evidence.evidenceScore(),
 1307:                 evidence.averageScore(),
 1308:                 evidence.averageConfidence(),
 1309:                 evidence.fiveMinute(),
 1310:                 evidence.oneHour());
 1311: 
 1312:         return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1313:                 "ACCUMULATED_LATE_ENTRY_DEFERRED",
 1314:                 "Accumulated evidence remains valid, but execution is deferred because "
 1315:                         + "Entry Quality is " + entryQuality.score() + "/100 ("
 1316:                         + entryQuality.classification() + "), accumulated fresh evidence "
 1317:                         + "contains no BUY observation, and fresh 5m setup context is "
 1318:                         + evidence.fiveMinute() + "."
 1319:                         + (entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh() == null
 1320:                                 ? ""
 1321:                                 : " A recent rejection zone near "
 1322:                                         + entryQuality.recentRejectedHigh()
 1323:                                         + " is only "
 1324:                                         + format(entryQuality.distanceFromRejectedHighPercent())
 1325:                                         + "% from the current entry price.")
 1326:                         + " The opportunity remains BUILDING for improved entry geometry "
 1327:                         + "or fresh BUY confirmation.",
 1328:                 evidence);
 1329:     }
 1330: 
 1331:     private String format(double value) {
 1332:         return String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.2f", value);
 1333:     }
 1334: 
 1335: 
 1336:     private ExecutionDecision exceptionalStrengthProbeDecision(
 1337:             TradeSignal current, Evidence e, HardRiskBlock block) {
 1338:         if (current == null || block == null || !block.blocked()) return null;
 1339:         if (!"BTC_CONTEXT_BLOCKED".equals(block.code())) return null;
 1340: 
 1341:         // BTC is allowed to reduce exposure here, not to manufacture extra score.
 1342:         // Every other risk authority remains mandatory.
 1343:         if (!current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1344:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
 1345:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 1346:                 || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1347:         if (current.getLatestPrice() == null || current.getStopLoss() == null || current.getTakeProfit() == null
 1348:                 || current.getStopLoss().signum() <= 0 || current.getTakeProfit().signum() <= 0) return null;
 1349: 
 1350:         if (current.getOriginalDecision() != SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) return null;
 1351:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && !isBullish(current.getDecision())) return null;
 1352:         if (current.getTotalScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_SCORE
 1353:                 || current.getTrendScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_TREND
 1354:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_MOMENTUM
 1355:                 || current.getVolumeScore() < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_VOLUME) return null;
 1356: 
 1357:         // This route is specifically a reversal probe, not a generic high-score bypass.
 1358:         // Require a fresh bearish -> exceptional bullish acceleration on the same 1m stream.
 1359:         TradeSignal prior = recentBearishReversalAnchor(current);
 1360:         if (prior == null) return null;
 1361:         SignalDecision priorEffective = strongerDecision(prior.getDecision(), prior.getOriginalDecision());
 1362:         int scoreJump = current.getTotalScore() - prior.getTotalScore();
 1363:         if (scoreJump < EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MIN_SCORE_JUMP) return null;
 1364: 
 1365:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1366:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1367:         if (five == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1368:         if (isBearish(five.getDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())
 1369:                 || isBearish(oneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1370: 
 1371:         SignalDecision btcDecision = current.getBtcContextDecision();
 1372:         int percent = btcDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL
 1373:                 ? EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_BTC_STRONG_SELL_PERCENT
 1374:                 : EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_BTC_SELL_PERCENT;
 1375: 
 1376:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1377:                 "EXCEPTIONAL_STRENGTH_PROBE",
 1378:                 "BTC_CONFLICT_REDUCED_PROBE",
 1379:                 percent,
 1380:                 "High-conviction reversal qualified for a reduced BTC-conflict probe: prior="
 1381:                         + priorEffective + " " + prior.getTotalScore() + " -> current="
 1382:                         + current.getOriginalDecision() + " " + current.getTotalScore()
 1383:                         + " (score jump +" + scoreJump + "), trend=" + current.getTrendScore()
 1384:                         + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore()
 1385:                         + ", 1m=" + current.getDecision() + ", 5m=" + five.getDecision()
 1386:                         + ", 1h=" + oneHour.getDecision() + ", BTC=" + (btcDecision == null ? "BEARISH" : btcDecision)
 1387:                         + ". BTC weakness reduces exposure to " + percent
 1388:                         + "% instead of increasing the technical score or bypassing asset-timeframe confirmation.",
 1389:                 e
 1390:         );
 1391:     }
 1392: 
 1393:     private ExecutionDecision applyExceptionalProbeEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
 1394:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
 1395:         // A probe is specifically for early reversal participation, but it must still
 1396:         // have a valid reward/risk plan and may not be an obvious late-stage chase.
 1397:         if (q.rewardRisk() <= 0d || q.score() < 40) {
 1398:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1399:                     "EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_PRICE_QUALITY_BLOCKED",
 1400:                     "Exceptional technical strength is present, but probe entry quality is only " + q.score()
 1401:                             + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "), expansion=" + format(q.expansionPercent())
 1402:                             + "%, ATR extension=" + format(q.atrExtension()) + ", R/R=" + format(q.rewardRisk()) + ".",
 1403:                     decision.evidence());
 1404:         }
 1405:         return decision;
 1406:     }
 1407: 
 1408:     private ExecutionDecision higherTimeframeTransitionDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1409:         if (current == null || current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1410:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) return null;
 1411:         if (current.getTotalScore() < TRANSITION_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE
 1412:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < TRANSITION_MIN_CURRENT_CONFIDENCE) return null;
 1413:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < TRANSITION_MIN_HEALTH) return null;
 1414: 
 1415:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1416:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1417:         if (five == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1418: 
 1419:         boolean fiveSupportive = (five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getDecision()))
 1420:                 && five.getTotalScore() >= TRANSITION_MIN_5M_SCORE
 1421:                 && five.getConfidenceScore() >= TRANSITION_MIN_5M_CONFIDENCE;
 1422:         boolean strongOneHour = isBullish(oneHour.getDecision())
 1423:                 && oneHour.getTotalScore() >= TRANSITION_MIN_1H_SCORE;
 1424:         if (!fiveSupportive || !strongOneHour) return null;
 1425:         if (isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())
 1426:                 || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1427:         if (!five.isStrategyEntryAllowed() || !five.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1428:                 || !five.isDerivativesEntryAllowed() || !five.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1429: 
 1430:         int percent = TRANSITION_POSITION_PERCENT;
 1431:         if (five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() > 0) {
 1432:             percent = Math.min(percent, five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent());
 1433:         }
 1434:         percent = Math.max(15, percent);
 1435: 
 1436:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1437:                 "HTF_TRANSITION",
 1438:                 "HTF_TRANSITION_REDUCED_ENTRY",
 1439:                 percent,
 1440:                 "Fresh higher-timeframe transition confirmed: 1h=" + oneHour.getDecision()
 1441:                         + " (score=" + oneHour.getTotalScore() + "), 5m=" + five.getDecision()
 1442:                         + " (score=" + five.getTotalScore() + ", confidence=" + five.getConfidenceScore() + ")"
 1443:                         + ", current 1m=" + current.getDecision()
 1444:                         + ", opportunity health=" + e.opportunityHealth() + "/100. "
 1445:                         + "ATR is extended, so execution is deliberately reduced to " + percent
 1446:                         + "% rather than rejecting the transition outright. Hard strategy/BTC/derivatives/liquidity vetoes remain absolute.",
 1447:                 e
 1448:         );
 1449:     }
 1450: 
 1451:     private ExecutionDecision applyTransitionEntryQualityGuard(ExecutionDecision decision, EntryQuality q) {
 1452:         if (decision == null || !decision.allowed()) return decision;
 1453:         // The transition route is specifically for a fast reversal where ATR can lag. We still
 1454:         // reject an extreme chase, but use a lower floor than the ordinary entry-quality guard.
 1455:         if (q.score() < 40 || q.rewardRisk() <= 0d) {
 1456:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1457:                     "TRANSITION_CHASE_BLOCKED",
 1458:                     "Higher-timeframe transition is valid, but price quality is too poor even for a reduced entry: "
 1459:                             + q.score() + "/100 (" + q.classification() + "), expansion="
 1460:                             + format(q.expansionPercent()) + "%, ATR extension=" + format(q.atrExtension())
 1461:                             + ", R/R=" + format(q.rewardRisk()) + ".",
 1462:                     decision.evidence());
 1463:         }
 1464:         int cap = q.score() >= 70 ? TRANSITION_POSITION_PERCENT : 20;
 1465:         int reduced = Math.min(decision.positionPercent(), cap);
 1466:         if (reduced == decision.positionPercent()) return decision;
 1467:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(decision.source(), decision.code(), reduced,
 1468:                 decision.explanation() + " Entry Quality " + q.score() + "/100 capped the transition allocation at "
 1469:                         + reduced + "%.", decision.evidence());
 1470:     }
 1471: 
 1472:     private TradeSignal setupTimeframeAtrAuthoritySignal(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1473:         if (current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1474: 
 1475:         // A neutral 1m candle is timing-neutral, not a bearish veto. A fresh 5m BUY
 1476:         // may still own setup-level ATR risk when opportunity evidence is already healthy.
 1477:         // Explicit bearish 1m information remains a veto for this fallback.
 1478:         if (isBearish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1479:         boolean supportiveCurrent = isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current);
 1480:         boolean healthyNeutralTiming = current.getDecision() == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL
 1481:                 && e.opportunityHealth() >= 60
 1482:                 && e.evidenceScore() >= MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE;
 1483:         if (!supportiveCurrent && !healthyNeutralTiming) return null;
 1484: 
 1485:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1486:         if (five == null || !isBullish(five.getDecision())) return null;
 1487:         if (!five.isFinalEntryAllowed() || !five.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1488: 
 1489:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1490:         if (oneHour != null && isBearish(oneHour.getDecision())) return null;
 1491:         return five;
 1492:     }
 1493: 
 1494:     private ExecutionDecision setupTimeframeAtrAuthorityDecision(
 1495:             TradeSignal current, Evidence e, TradeSignal five) {
 1496:         int fiveRecommended = five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() > 0
 1497:                 ? five.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent()
 1498:                 : DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT;
 1499:         int percent = Math.min(DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT, fiveRecommended);
 1500: 
 1501:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1502:                 "SETUP_TIMEFRAME_ATR",
 1503:                 "REDUCED_POSITION_ALLOWED",
 1504:                 percent,
 1505:                 "The current 1m signal is ATR-extended, but the fresh 5m BUY remains "
 1506:                         + "the setup-risk authority and its ATR plan explicitly allows immediate entry at "
 1507:                         + fiveRecommended + "%. Execution is therefore allowed at a conservative "
 1508:                         + percent + "% allocation instead of permanently rejecting the opportunity. "
 1509:                         + "A neutral 1m candle is treated as timing-neutral; explicit bearish 1m/1h context still vetoes. "
 1510:                         + "Entry Quality is evaluated with the authoritative 5m ATR so a tiny 1m ATR cannot manufacture "
 1511:                         + "a false late-stage chase classification.",
 1512:                 e
 1513:         );
 1514:     }
 1515: 
 1516:     private ExecutionDecision breakoutRetracementDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1517:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || current.getLatestPrice() == null) return null;
 1518:         if (isBearish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1519:         if (!current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed() || !current.isFinalEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1520:         if (current.getTotalScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE
 1521:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_CONFIDENCE
 1522:                 || current.getTrendScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_TREND
 1523:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1524:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_HEALTH) return null;
 1525: 
 1526:         TradeSignal origin = recentBreakoutRetracementOrigin(current);
 1527:         if (origin == null || origin.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() == null
 1528:                 || origin.getAtrAtSignal() == null || origin.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) return null;
 1529: 
 1530:         BigDecimal target = origin.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice();
 1531:         BigDecimal atr = origin.getAtrAtSignal();
