 1532:         BigDecimal upper = target.add(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_TARGET_TOLERANCE_ATR)));
 1533:         BigDecimal lower = target.subtract(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MAX_OVERSHOOT_ATR)));
 1534:         BigDecimal price = current.getLatestPrice();
 1535:         if (price.compareTo(upper) > 0 || price.compareTo(lower) < 0) return null;
 1536: 
 1537:         EntryQuality currentQuality = assessEntryQuality(current);
 1538:         if (currentQuality.score() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
 1539:                 || currentQuality.rewardRisk() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_RR) return null;
 1540: 
 1541:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1542:         if (five == null || isBearish(five.getDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1543:         if (!(five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getDecision())
 1544:                 || five.getOriginalDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getOriginalDecision()))) return null;
 1545:         if (five.getMomentumScore() < BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_5M_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1546: 
 1547:         // A bearish 1h may be the old trend the breakout is trying to reverse. It is
 1548:         // allowed only when it already existed at the breakout origin and has not been
 1549:         // replaced by a fresh/worsening bearish snapshot after that origin.
 1550:         TradeSignal originOneHour = latestAtOrBefore(origin, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1551:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1552:         boolean oneHourBearish = oneHour != null
 1553:                 && (isBearish(oneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision()));
 1554:         if (oneHourBearish) {
 1555:             boolean bearishAtOrigin = originOneHour != null
 1556:                     && (isBearish(originOneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(originOneHour.getOriginalDecision()));
 1557:             boolean freshBearishAfterOrigin = oneHour.getGeneratedAt() != null
 1558:                     && oneHour.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(origin.getGeneratedAt());
 1559:             if (!bearishAtOrigin || freshBearishAfterOrigin || isOneHourBearishWorsening(oneHour, current)) return null;
 1560:         }
 1561: 
 1562:         int percent = oneHourBearish
 1563:                 ? BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_BEARISH_1H_POSITION_PERCENT
 1564:                 : BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_POSITION_PERCENT;
 1565: 
 1566:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1567:                 "BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT",
 1568:                 "BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_ENTRY",
 1569:                 percent,
 1570:                 "A recent BREAKOUT was deliberately deferred by ATR instead of being chased, and price has now "
 1571:                         + "returned to the requested retracement zone. origin=" + origin.getLatestPrice()
 1572:                         + ", target=" + target + ", current=" + price
 1573:                         + ". Breakout confirmation remains valid (score=" + origin.getTotalScore()
 1574:                         + ", trend=" + origin.getTrendScore() + ", momentum=" + origin.getMomentumScore()
 1575:                         + ", volume=" + origin.getVolumeScore() + "). Current pullback quality remains supportive "
 1576:                         + "(score=" + current.getTotalScore() + ", confidence=" + current.getConfidenceScore()
 1577:                         + ", trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore()
 1578:                         + ", health=" + e.opportunityHealth() + ", 5m=" + five.getDecision()
 1579:                         + ", 5m momentum=" + five.getMomentumScore() + ", Entry Quality=" + currentQuality.score()
 1580:                         + "/100, R/R=" + format(currentQuality.rewardRisk()) + "). Current-candle volume is not "
 1581:                         + "required to repeat the breakout spike; the original breakout already supplied that evidence. "
 1582:                         + "Execute only a reduced " + percent + "% position; normal BUY and SELL logic are unchanged.",
 1583:                 e);
 1584:     }
 1585: 
 1586:     private TradeSignal recentBreakoutRetracementOrigin(TradeSignal current) {
 1587:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_LOOKBACK);
 1588:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1589:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1590:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1591:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1592:                 .filter(s -> s.getSelectedStrategy() == TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT
 1593:                         && s.getMarketRegime() == MarketRegime.BREAKOUT)
 1594:                 .filter(s -> "WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT".equals(s.getAtrEntryType()))
 1595:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed())
 1596:                 .filter(s -> s.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() != null
 1597:                         && s.getAtrAtSignal() != null && s.getAtrAtSignal().signum() > 0)
 1598:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_SCORE
 1599:                         && s.getTrendScore() >= BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_TREND
 1600:                         && s.getVolumeScore() >= BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_VOLUME
 1601:                         && s.getMomentumScore() >= BREAKOUT_RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_MOMENTUM)
 1602:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed() && s.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1603:                         && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed() && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1604:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1605:                 .max(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
 1606:                 .orElse(null);
 1607:     }
 1608: 
 1609:     private ExecutionDecision reversalRetracementDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1610:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null || current.getLatestPrice() == null) return null;
 1611:         if (isBearish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1612: 
 1613:         TradeSignal origin = recentAtrRetracementOrigin(current);
 1614:         if (origin == null || origin.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() == null
 1615:                 || origin.getAtrAtSignal() == null || origin.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) return null;
 1616: 
 1617:         BigDecimal target = origin.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice();
 1618:         BigDecimal atr = origin.getAtrAtSignal();
 1619:         BigDecimal upper = target.add(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(RETRACEMENT_TARGET_TOLERANCE_ATR)));
 1620:         BigDecimal lower = target.subtract(atr.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(RETRACEMENT_MAX_OVERSHOOT_ATR)));
 1621:         BigDecimal price = current.getLatestPrice();
 1622:         if (price.compareTo(upper) > 0 || price.compareTo(lower) < 0) return null;
 1623: 
 1624:         // The retracement route may preserve an old high-quality thesis, but it may not
 1625:         // resurrect an opportunity whose current health/risk economics have already decayed.
 1626:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_HEALTH) return null;
 1627:         if (current.getStopLoss() == null || current.getTakeProfit() == null
 1628:                 || current.getStopLoss().signum() <= 0 || current.getTakeProfit().signum() <= 0) return null;
 1629:         EntryQuality currentQuality = assessEntryQuality(current);
 1630:         if (currentQuality.score() < RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_ENTRY_QUALITY
 1631:                 || currentQuality.rewardRisk() < RETRACEMENT_MIN_CURRENT_RR) return null;
 1632: 
 1633:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1634:         if (five == null || isBearish(five.getDecision()) || isBearish(five.getOriginalDecision())) return null;
 1635:         if (five.getMomentumScore() < RETRACEMENT_MIN_5M_MOMENTUM
 1636:                 || five.getMacdScore() < RETRACEMENT_MIN_5M_MACD_SCORE
 1637:                 || five.getRsiScore() < RETRACEMENT_MIN_5M_RSI_SCORE) return null;
 1638: 
 1639:         // Preserve the distinction between an OLD bearish 1h regime that the reversal is
 1640:         // trying to turn, and a NEW bearish 1h signal that appeared after the origin BUY.
 1641:         // A newly bearish 1h invalidates the pending setup. A pre-existing bearish 1h may
 1642:         // remain only if its severity/score structure is not worsening, and it caps size.
 1643:         TradeSignal originOneHour = latestAtOrBefore(origin, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1644:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1645:         boolean currentOneHourBearish = oneHour != null
 1646:                 && (isBearish(oneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision()));
 1647:         if (currentOneHourBearish) {
 1648:             boolean bearishWasPresentAtOrigin = originOneHour != null
 1649:                     && (isBearish(originOneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(originOneHour.getOriginalDecision()));
 1650:             boolean newBearishSnapshotAfterOrigin = oneHour.getGeneratedAt() != null
 1651:                     && oneHour.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(origin.getGeneratedAt());
 1652:             if (!bearishWasPresentAtOrigin || newBearishSnapshotAfterOrigin
 1653:                     || isOneHourBearishWorsening(oneHour, current)) return null;
 1654:         }
 1655: 
 1656:         if (!current.isStrategyEntryAllowed() || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
 1657:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed() || !current.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1658: 
 1659:         int percent = RETRACEMENT_POSITION_PERCENT;
 1660:         if (oneHour != null && (isBearish(oneHour.getDecision()) || isBearish(oneHour.getOriginalDecision()))) {
 1661:             percent = 15;
 1662:         }
 1663: 
 1664:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1665:                 "REVERSAL_RETRACEMENT",
 1666:                 "ATR_RETRACEMENT_REACHED",
 1667:                 percent,
 1668:                 "ATR correctly deferred the original BUY instead of chasing it. Price has now returned to the "
 1669:                         + "ATR retracement zone: origin=" + origin.getLatestPrice()
 1670:                         + ", target=" + target + ", current=" + price
 1671:                         + ". Original quality remained high (score=" + origin.getTotalScore()
 1672:                         + ", trend=" + origin.getTrendScore() + ", volume=" + origin.getVolumeScore()
 1673:                         + ") and the latest as-of 5m context is constructive (decision=" + five.getDecision()
 1674:                         + ", momentum=" + five.getMomentumScore() + ", MACD score=" + five.getMacdScore()
 1675:                         + ", RSI score=" + five.getRsiScore() + "). "
 1676:                         + (oneHour == null ? "No fresh 1h replay context is available; no bullish 1h state was invented. "
 1677:                         : "1h=" + oneHour.getDecision() + " and is a pre-existing, non-worsening context. ")
 1678:                         + "Current opportunity health=" + e.opportunityHealth()
 1679:                         + ", Entry Quality=" + currentQuality.score() + "/100, current R/R=" + format(currentQuality.rewardRisk()) + ". "
 1680:                         + "Execute only a reduced " + percent + "% retracement position; normal BUY logic is unchanged.",
 1681:                 e);
 1682:     }
 1683: 
 1684:     private TradeSignal recentAtrRetracementOrigin(TradeSignal current) {
 1685:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(RETRACEMENT_SETUP_LOOKBACK);
 1686:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1687:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1688:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1689:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1690:                 .filter(s -> isBullish(s.getOriginalDecision()) || isBullish(s.getDecision()))
 1691:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_SCORE
 1692:                         && s.getTrendScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_TREND
 1693:                         && s.getVolumeScore() >= RETRACEMENT_MIN_ORIGIN_VOLUME)
 1694:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed() && s.isAtrOverextended())
 1695:                 .filter(s -> s.getAtrRetracementEntryPrice() != null && s.getAtrAtSignal() != null)
 1696:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed() && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed() && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1697:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1698:                 .max(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
 1699:                 .orElse(null);
 1700:     }
 1701: 
 1702:     private boolean isOneHourBearishWorsening(TradeSignal latest, TradeSignal current) {
 1703:         if (latest == null || current == null) return false;
 1704:         List<TradeSignal> hours = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), TREND_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1705:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt() != null && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(latest.getGeneratedAt()))
 1706:                 .toList();
 1707:         TradeSignal previous = hours.isEmpty() ? null : hours.get(0);
 1708:         if (previous == null) return false;
 1709: 
 1710:         SignalDecision latestEffective = strongerDecision(latest.getDecision(), latest.getOriginalDecision());
 1711:         SignalDecision previousEffective = strongerDecision(previous.getDecision(), previous.getOriginalDecision());
 1712:         boolean severityWorsened = bearishSeverity(latestEffective) > bearishSeverity(previousEffective);
 1713:         return severityWorsened
 1714:                 || latest.getTotalScore() < previous.getTotalScore()
 1715:                 || latest.getTrendScore() < previous.getTrendScore()
 1716:                 || latest.getMomentumScore() < previous.getMomentumScore();
 1717:     }
 1718: 
 1719:     private int bearishSeverity(SignalDecision decision) {
 1720:         if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) return 2;
 1721:         if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) return 1;
 1722:         return 0;
 1723:     }
 1724: 
 1725:     private ExecutionDecision deferredContinuationDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1726:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) return null;
 1727:         ImmediatePriceAction priceAction = immediatePriceAction(current);
 1728:         if (!priceAction.supportive()) {
 1729:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1730:                     "IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_REVALIDATION_REQUIRED",
 1731:                     "Deferred continuation stayed technically supportive, but the latest fully closed 1m price action "
 1732:                             + "already rejected the impulse (" + priceAction.summary() + "). Wait for fresh price "
 1733:                             + "support instead of chasing stale derived evidence.",
 1734:                     e);
 1735:         }
 1736:         // Do not require finalEntryAllowed/atrImmediateEntryAllowed here. The aggregate final
 1737:         // entry flag may be false solely because ATR requested a pullback; that is exactly
 1738:         // the condition this continuation route is designed to reassess. All non-ATR hard
 1739:         // vetoes were already enforced before this method is called.
 1740:         if (current.getTotalScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_CURRENT_SCORE
 1741:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_CURRENT_CONFIDENCE) return null;
 1742:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < DEFERRED_MIN_HEALTH
 1743:                 || e.evidenceMomentum() < DEFERRED_MIN_EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1744:         if (e.fiveMinute() == null || e.oneHour() == null
 1745:                 || isBearish(e.fiveMinute()) || isBearish(e.oneHour())) return null;
 1746: 
 1747:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1748:         if (five == null || !(five.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(five.getDecision()))
 1749:                 || five.getTotalScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_5M_SCORE
 1750:                 || five.getConfidenceScore() < DEFERRED_MIN_5M_CONFIDENCE) return null;
 1751:         if (!(e.oneHour() == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(e.oneHour()))) return null;
 1752: 
 1753:         TradeSignal deferredBuy = priorAtrDeferredBuy(current);
 1754:         if (deferredBuy == null) return null;
 1755: 
 1756:         double rewardRisk = currentRewardRisk(current);
 1757:         if (rewardRisk < 1.0d) {
 1758:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1759:                     "CONTINUATION_RISK_REWARD_LOW",
 1760:                     "A prior ATR-deferred BUY exists and continuation is confirmed, but the current reward/risk is only "
 1761:                             + String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.2f", rewardRisk)
 1762:                             + ". The engine will not chase a continuation with less than 1:1 current reward/risk.",
 1763:                     e);
 1764:         }
 1765: 
 1766:         int percent = Math.min(DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT,
 1767:                 current.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent() > 0
 1768:                         ? current.getAtrRecommendedPositionPercent()
 1769:                         : DEFERRED_POSITION_PERCENT);
 1770:         percent = Math.max(20, percent);
 1771: 
 1772:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1773:                 "DEFERRED_CONTINUATION",
 1774:                 "BREAKOUT_CONTINUATION_ENTRY",
 1775:                 percent,
 1776:                 "A prior 1m BUY was deferred only by ATR pullback logic, but the pullback did not arrive. "
 1777:                         + "Fresh continuation is now supported by current 1m evidence, 5m=" + e.fiveMinute()
 1778:                         + " (score=" + five.getTotalScore() + ", confidence=" + five.getConfidenceScore() + ")"
 1779:                         + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + ", opportunity health=" + e.opportunityHealth()
 1780:                         + ", evidence momentum=" + e.evidenceMomentum() + ". "
 1781:                         + "Execution uses the current stop/target and a reduced " + percent + "% position.",
 1782:                 e);
 1783:     }
 1784: 
 1785:     private TradeSignal priorAtrDeferredBuy(TradeSignal current) {
 1786:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(DEFERRED_BUY_LOOKBACK);
 1787:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1788:                 .filter(s -> s.getGeneratedAt() != null
 1789:                         && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1790:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 1791:                 .filter(s -> isBullish(s.getDecision()) || isBullish(s.getOriginalDecision()))
 1792:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= properties.minimumBuyScore())
 1793:                 .filter(s -> !s.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed())
 1794:                 .filter(s -> s.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1795:                         && s.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1796:                         && s.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()
 1797:                         && s.isLiquidityEntryAllowed())
 1798:                 .filter(s -> {
 1799:                     String type = s.getAtrEntryType();
 1800:                     return "PULLBACK_ENTRY".equals(type) || "WAIT_FOR_RETRACEMENT".equals(type);
 1801:                 })
 1802:                 .filter(s -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(s, "DEFERRED_ORIGIN"))
 1803:                 .findFirst()
 1804:                 .orElse(null);
 1805:     }
 1806: 
 1807:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal) {
 1808:         return currentRewardRisk(signal, signal == null ? null : signal.getLatestPrice());
 1809:     }
 1810: 
 1811:     private double currentRewardRisk(TradeSignal signal, BigDecimal marketPrice) {
 1812:         if (signal == null || marketPrice == null || signal.getStopLoss() == null || signal.getTakeProfit() == null) return 0d;
 1813:         java.math.BigDecimal risk = marketPrice.subtract(signal.getStopLoss()).abs();
 1814:         java.math.BigDecimal reward = signal.getTakeProfit().subtract(marketPrice);
 1815:         if (risk.signum() <= 0 || reward.signum() <= 0) return 0d;
 1816:         return reward.divide(risk, 8, java.math.RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 1817:     }
 1818: 
 1819:     /**
 1820:      * Small exploratory entry for a proven pressure-reversal sequence while normal MTF
 1821:      * confirmation is still lagging. This is intentionally NOT a BUY-promotion method.
 1822:      *
 1823:      * <p>Normal direct BUY validation already ran before this method. Therefore a BUY
 1824:      * that belongs to the existing normal path can never use PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY as a
 1825:      * fallback around normal validation. The probe is restricted to WATCH/NEUTRAL.</p>
 1826:      *
 1827:      * <p>A bearish 1h label is allowed only because the exposure is capped at 15% and
 1828:      * PressureReadinessService must prove a completed burst/rejection/higher-low/rebuild
 1829:      * sequence using only candles closed as-of the current signal timestamp. This does
 1830:      * not weaken the normal 1h veto for normal-sized trades.</p>
 1831:      */
 1832: 
 1833:     /**
 1834:      * FIX-026 recovery-transition probe. Normal direct BUY and the proven pressure-probe
 1835:      * route already had priority before this method. This is intentionally a small,
 1836:      * state-transition exception for WATCH/NEUTRAL only; it never converts a bearish
 1837:      * current signal and it never bypasses a hard risk/context veto.
 1838:      */
 1839:     private ExecutionDecision recoveryTransitionDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence evidence) {
 1840:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 1841:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) return null;
 1842:         if (current.getTotalScore() < 72 || current.getConfidenceScore() < 68
 1843:                 || current.getTrendScore() < 20 || current.getMomentumScore() < 14) return null;
 1844: 
 1845:         // Keep all production hard authorities intact. The ENA case had every one of these
 1846:         // gates open; this probe must not manufacture permission when ATR, liquidity, BTC,
 1847:         // derivatives, confluence, strategy or FinalDecision has already vetoed entry.
 1848:         if (!current.isFinalEntryAllowed() || !current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1849:                 || !current.isConfluenceEntryAllowed() || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1850:                 || !current.isBtcContextEntryAllowed() || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 1851:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1852: 
 1853:         Instant bearishCutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(Duration.ofMinutes(10));
 1854:         TradeSignal recentBearish = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1855:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null && s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt()))
 1856:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(bearishCutoff))
 1857:                 .filter(s -> isBearish(strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision())))
 1858:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() <= 35)
 1859:                 .findFirst().orElse(null);
 1860:         if (recentBearish == null) return null;
 1861: 
 1862:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1863:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1864:         if (five == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1865:         SignalDecision fiveDecision = strongerDecision(five.getDecision(), five.getOriginalDecision());
 1866:         SignalDecision oneHourDecision = strongerDecision(oneHour.getDecision(), oneHour.getOriginalDecision());
 1867:         if (isBearish(fiveDecision) || isBearish(oneHourDecision)) return null;
 1868: 
 1869:         RecoveryTransitionService.Result recovery = recoveryTransitionService.evaluate(current);
 1870:         if (recovery == null || !recovery.probeReady()) return null;
 1871: 
 1872:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1873:                 "RECOVERY_TRANSITION_ENTRY",
 1874:                 "ABSORPTION_RECOVERY_PROBE",
 1875:                 25,
 1876:                 "Recovery-transition probe approved after recent bearish signal #" + recentBearish.getId()
 1877:                         + " (score=" + recentBearish.getTotalScore() + ") recovered into current "
 1878:                         + current.getDecision() + " " + current.getTotalScore()
 1879:                         + " (trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore()
 1880:                         + "). " + recovery.explanation() + " 5m=" + fiveDecision
 1881:                         + ", 1h=" + oneHourDecision + ". Exposure is capped at 25% until ordinary confirmation adds.",
 1882:                 evidence);
 1883:     }
 1884: 
 1885:     private ExecutionDecision pressureProbeDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1886:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 1887: 
 1888:         // Preserve normal BUY authority. A bullish final decision belongs to the normal
 1889:         // path and must never fall back to this probe if normal validation rejects it.
 1890:         if (isBullish(current.getDecision()) || isBearish(current.getDecision())) return null;
 1891:         if (current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.WATCH && current.getDecision() != SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) return null;
 1892: 
 1893:         if (current.getTotalScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_SCORE
 1894:                 || current.getConfidenceScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_CONFIDENCE
 1895:                 || current.getTrendScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_TREND
 1896:                 || current.getVolumeScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_VOLUME
 1897:                 || current.getMomentumScore() < PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_1M_MOMENTUM) return null;
 1898: 
 1899:         // These are existing production safety authorities; the pressure path cannot
 1900:         // manufacture permission when another context/risk layer already vetoed entry.
 1901:         if (!current.isFinalEntryAllowed()
 1902:                 || !current.isStrategyEntryAllowed()
 1903:                 || !current.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 1904:                 || !current.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()
 1905:                 || !current.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 1906:                 || !current.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) return null;
 1907: 
 1908:         PressureReadinessService.Result pressure = pressureReadinessService.evaluate(current);
 1909:         if (pressure == null || !pressure.probeReady()) return null;
 1910: 
 1911:         TradeSignal latestFive = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 1912:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 1913:         if (latestFive == null || oneHour == null) return null;
 1914: 
 1915:         SignalDecision latestFiveDecision = strongerDecision(latestFive.getDecision(), latestFive.getOriginalDecision());
 1916:         SignalDecision oneHourDecision = strongerDecision(oneHour.getDecision(), oneHour.getOriginalDecision());
 1917: 
 1918:         // A fresh 5m STRONG_SELL is too hostile even for a probe. A plain 5m SELL can
 1919:         // represent the retest only when a recent 5m WATCH/BUY BREAKOUT already proved
 1920:         // the first bullish attempt. This is the SOL sequence: setup -> retest -> rebuild.
 1921:         if (latestFiveDecision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) return null;
 1922: 
 1923:         Instant setupCutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(PRESSURE_RECENT_5M_SETUP_LOOKBACK);
 1924:         TradeSignal recentFiveMinuteSetup = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 1925:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 1926:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())
 1927:                         && !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(setupCutoff))
 1928:                 .filter(s -> {
 1929:                     SignalDecision d = strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision());
 1930:                     return d == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(d);
 1931:                 })
 1932:                 .filter(s -> s.getSelectedStrategy() == TradingStrategy.BREAKOUT)
 1933:                 .filter(s -> s.getTotalScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_SCORE)
 1934:                 .filter(s -> s.getVolumeScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_VOLUME)
 1935:                 .filter(s -> s.getMomentumScore() >= PRESSURE_PROBE_MIN_5M_SETUP_MOMENTUM)
 1936:                 .findFirst()
 1937:                 .orElse(null);
 1938:         if (recentFiveMinuteSetup == null) return null;
 1939: 
 1940:         // The probe may coexist with a bearish 1h because it is explicitly counter-trend
 1941:         // discovery risk, not normal confirmation. We still require a fresh 1h object so
 1942:         // the conflict is known/auditable rather than silently treated as unavailable.
 1943:         if (oneHourDecision == null) return null;
 1944: 
 1945:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 1946:                 "PRESSURE_PROBE_ENTRY",
 1947:                 "ABSORPTION_RETEST_REBUILD_PROBE",
 1948:                 PRESSURE_PROBE_POSITION_PERCENT,
 1949:                 "Small pressure probe approved without changing the normal BUY path. "
 1950:                         + pressure.explanation()
 1951:                         + " Current 1m=" + current.getDecision() + " " + current.getTotalScore()
 1952:                         + " (trend=" + current.getTrendScore() + ", volume=" + current.getVolumeScore()
 1953:                         + ", momentum=" + current.getMomentumScore() + "), latest 5m="
 1954:                         + latestFiveDecision + " " + latestFive.getTotalScore()
 1955:                         + ", prior 5m setup #" + recentFiveMinuteSetup.getId() + "="
 1956:                         + strongerDecision(recentFiveMinuteSetup.getDecision(), recentFiveMinuteSetup.getOriginalDecision())
 1957:                         + " " + recentFiveMinuteSetup.getTotalScore() + " " + recentFiveMinuteSetup.getSelectedStrategy()
 1958:                         + ", 1h=" + oneHourDecision + " " + oneHour.getTotalScore()
 1959:                         + ". Exposure is capped at " + PRESSURE_PROBE_POSITION_PERCENT
 1960:                         + "% until the existing progressive/normal confirmation path adds or exits.",
 1961:                 e);
 1962:     }
 1963: 
 1964:     private ExecutionDecision accumulatedDecision(TradeSignal current, Evidence e) {
 1965:         if (!isSupportiveCurrentSignal(current)) {
 1966:             return ExecutionDecision.observe("NO_BULLISH_EVIDENCE",
 1967:                     "Current 1m signal does not add BUY/WATCH evidence.", e);
 1968:         }
 1969:         ImmediatePriceAction priceAction = immediatePriceAction(current);
 1970:         if (!priceAction.supportive()) {
 1971:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 1972:                     "IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_REVALIDATION_REQUIRED",
 1973:                     "Accumulated evidence remains valid, but the latest fully closed 1m candle no longer confirms an "
 1974:                             + "immediate entry (" + priceAction.summary() + "). The opportunity stays alive and must "
 1975:                             + "wait for fresh price support; WEAK_UPTREND and ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE are not reclassified.",
 1976:                     e);
 1977:         }
 1978:         if (e.fiveMinute() == null || e.oneHour() == null) {
 1979:             return ExecutionDecision.building("MISSING_CONTEXT",
 1980:                     "Opportunity is building, but fresh 5m/1h context is not yet available.", e);
 1981:         }
 1982:         if (isBearish(e.fiveMinute()) || isBearish(e.oneHour())) {
 1983:             return ExecutionDecision.reject("HIGHER_TIMEFRAME_BEARISH",
 1984:                     "Opportunity cancelled because 5m or 1h context is bearish.", e);
 1985:         }
 1986:         if (e.opportunityHealth() < 40) {
 1987:             return ExecutionDecision.building("OPPORTUNITY_RECOVERING",
 1988:                     "Bullish evidence is returning, but opportunity health is only "
 1989:                             + e.opportunityHealth() + "/100 after recent bearish/aging penalties.", e);
 1990:         }
 1991:         if (e.evidenceScore() < MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE) {
 1992:             return ExecutionDecision.building("EVIDENCE_BUILDING",
 1993:                     "Bullish evidence is accumulating: score " + e.evidenceScore() + "/" + MIN_EVIDENCE_SCORE
 1994:                             + " from " + e.buyCount() + " BUY and " + e.watchCount() + " WATCH observations.", e);
 1995:         }
 1996:         if (e.buyCount() == 0 && e.watchCount() < 5) {
 1997:             return ExecutionDecision.building("WATCH_ONLY_BUILDING",
 1998:                     "WATCH evidence is persistent but has not yet produced a BUY signal; continuing to observe.", e);
 1999:         }
 2000: 
 2001:         // WATCH-only accumulation is intentionally allowed to keep building across symbols,
 2002:         // but old WATCH observations must not manufacture a fresh BUY when the current
 2003:         // higher-timeframe context is only 1h WATCH + 5m NEUTRAL. In that state there is
 2004:         // no fresh setup confirmation: keep the opportunity memory, but wait for either a
 2005:         // 5m WATCH/BUY or a genuine 1h BUY before committing capital. Existing routes with
 2006:         // real BUY evidence are unchanged, as are HTF_TRANSITION / IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION.
 2007:         if (e.buyCount() == 0
 2008:                 && !isBullish(e.oneHour())
 2009:                 && e.fiveMinute() != SignalDecision.WATCH
 2010:                 && !isBullish(e.fiveMinute())) {
 2011:             return ExecutionDecision.building("WATCH_ONLY_NEEDS_FRESH_CONFIRMATION",
 2012:                     "Accumulated WATCH evidence remains valid, but there is still no BUY observation and fresh "
 2013:                             + "higher-timeframe confirmation is missing (5m=" + e.fiveMinute()
 2014:                             + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour() + "). Wait for 5m WATCH/BUY or 1h BUY before entry.", e);
 2015:         }
 2016:         if (e.averageScore() < 65 || e.averageConfidence() < 65) {
 2017:             return ExecutionDecision.building("EVIDENCE_QUALITY_LOW",
 2018:                     "Evidence persists, but average quality is still low: score=" + e.averageScore()
 2019:                             + ", confidence=" + e.averageConfidence() + ".", e);
 2020:         }
 2021: 
 2022:         // FIX-091 / Fix 8: accumulated evidence is also forbidden from entering after the
 2023:         // current setup has materially deteriorated from the recent opportunity peak. This
 2024:         // directly protects the UNI/PEPE pattern where a strong BUY peak was followed by a
 2025:         // weaker WATCH that later executed only because old evidence remained in memory.
 2026:         EvidenceDeterioration deterioration = assessEvidenceDeterioration(current);
 2027:         if (deterioration.material()) {
 2028:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 2029:                     "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE_DETERIORATED",
 2030:                     deterioration.explanation(),
 2031:                     e);
 2032:         }
 2033: 
 2034:         // FIX-021 / BICOUSDT + ETHUSDT:
 2035:         // Accumulated evidence is memory, not a separate execution authority. It must obey
 2036:         // the SAME configured 5m/1h profile that a normal direct BUY would face. This closes
 2037:         // the contradiction where BALANCED rejected 5m WATCH + 1h NEUTRAL for a fresh BUY,
 2038:         // but ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE later entered with that same or weaker HTF state.
 2039:         TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult authority = validationService.validateBuyContext(current);
 2040:         if (!authority.allowed()) {
 2041:             return ExecutionDecision.building("ACCUMULATED_AUTHORITY_WAIT",
 2042:                     "Accumulated evidence remains stored, but current HTF execution authority is insufficient: "
 2043:                             + authority.code() + " - " + authority.explanation(), e);
 2044:         }
 2045: 
 2046:         int percent = e.evidenceScore() >= STRONG_EVIDENCE_SCORE ? 50 : 25;
 2047:         if (e.buyCount() >= 2) percent += 10;
 2048:         if (e.fiveMinute() == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 2049:         if (isBullish(e.fiveMinute())) percent += 15;
 2050:         if (e.oneHour() == SignalDecision.WATCH) percent += 5;
 2051:         if (isBullish(e.oneHour())) percent += 10;
 2052:         // Never size accumulated evidence above the authority granted by the configured
 2053:         // direct-BUY profile. Historical evidence may reduce uncertainty, not increase HTF authority.
 2054:         percent = Math.min(Math.min(75, percent), authority.positionPercent());
 2055: 
 2056:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 2057:                 "ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE",
 2058:                 "OPPORTUNITY_CONFIRMED",
 2059:                 percent,
 2060:                 "Execution Intelligence approved the current signal from accumulated fresh evidence: "
 2061:                         + e.buyCount() + " BUY, " + e.watchCount() + " WATCH, evidence score=" + e.evidenceScore()
 2062:                         + ", average score=" + e.averageScore() + ", average confidence=" + e.averageConfidence()
 2063:                         + ", 5m=" + e.fiveMinute() + ", 1h=" + e.oneHour()
 2064:                         + ", HTF authority=" + authority.code()
 2065:                         + ". No second trade signal was generated.",
 2066:                 e
 2067:         );
 2068:     }
 2069: 
 2070: 
 2071:     /**
 2072:      * BALANCED_EARLY deliberately bypasses accumulated evidence in normal conditions.
 2073:      * After a fresh 5m SELL/STRONG_SELL, however, it must prove that the bearish phase
 2074:      * actually recovered before that shortcut is available again. This guard changes
 2075:      * only BALANCED_EARLY; accumulated evidence, normal BUY, retracement, transition,
 2076:      * ATR and SELL logic remain unchanged.
 2077:      */
 2078:     private ExecutionDecision balancedEarlyPostBearishGuard(
 2079:             TradeSignal current, Evidence evidence, EntryQuality entryQuality,
 2080:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validation) {
 2081:         if (validation == null || !validation.allowed() || !"BALANCED_EARLY".equals(validation.code())) return null;
 2082:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 2083: 
 2084:         List<TradeSignal> fiveMinuteHistory = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 2085:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null && !s.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt()))
 2086:                 .sorted(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 2087:                 .toList();
 2088: 
 2089:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(BALANCED_EARLY_BEARISH_LOOKBACK);
 2090:         TradeSignal lastBearishFive = fiveMinuteHistory.stream()
 2091:                 .filter(s -> !s.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 2092:                 .filter(s -> isBearish(strongerDecision(s.getDecision(), s.getOriginalDecision())))
 2093:                 .findFirst()
 2094:                 .orElse(null);
 2095:         if (lastBearishFive == null) return null; // existing BALANCED_EARLY behavior stays untouched
 2096: 
 2097:         int consecutiveRecoveredFive = 0;
 2098:         for (TradeSignal five : fiveMinuteHistory) {
 2099:             if (!five.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(lastBearishFive.getGeneratedAt())) break;
 2100:             SignalDecision decision = strongerDecision(five.getDecision(), five.getOriginalDecision());
 2101:             if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(decision)) consecutiveRecoveredFive++;
 2102:             else break;
 2103:         }
 2104: 
 2105:         boolean healthOk = evidence.opportunityHealth() >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_HEALTH;
 2106:         boolean evidenceOk = evidence.evidenceScore() >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_EVIDENCE;
 2107:         boolean recoveryOk = consecutiveRecoveredFive >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_REQUIRED_5M_RECOVERY;
 2108:         boolean qualityOk = entryQuality != null && entryQuality.score() >= BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY;
 2109: 
 2110:         if (!healthOk || !evidenceOk || !recoveryOk || !qualityOk) {
 2111:             return ExecutionDecision.building(
 2112:                     "BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
 2113:                     "BALANCED_EARLY is paused after a fresh 5m bearish confirmation. "
 2114:                             + "Require health>=" + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_HEALTH
 2115:                             + ", evidence>=" + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_EVIDENCE
 2116:                             + ", " + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_REQUIRED_5M_RECOVERY
 2117:                             + " consecutive recovered 5m WATCH/BUY states, and Entry Quality>="
 2118:                             + BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY
 2119:                             + ". Actual health=" + evidence.opportunityHealth()
 2120:                             + ", evidence=" + evidence.evidenceScore()
 2121:                             + ", recovered5m=" + consecutiveRecoveredFive
 2122:                             + ", entryQuality=" + (entryQuality == null ? 0 : entryQuality.score()) + ".",
 2123:                     evidence);
 2124:         }
 2125: 
 2126:         int reduced = Math.min(validation.positionPercent(), BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_MAX_POSITION);
 2127:         return ExecutionDecision.allow(
 2128:                 "IMMEDIATE_VALIDATION",
 2129:                 "BALANCED_EARLY_POST_BEARISH_RECOVERED",
 2130:                 reduced,
 2131:                 "BALANCED_EARLY recovered safely after recent 5m bearish context: health="
 2132:                         + evidence.opportunityHealth() + ", evidence=" + evidence.evidenceScore()
 2133:                         + ", recovered5m=" + consecutiveRecoveredFive + ", Entry Quality="
 2134:                         + entryQuality.score() + ". Initial exposure capped at " + reduced
 2135:                         + "% so normal progressive confirmation can add later.",
 2136:                 evidence);
 2137:     }
 2138: 
 2139:     private boolean isDirectBuyCandidate(TradeSignal signal) {
 2140:         return signal.isFinalEntryAllowed()
 2141:                 && signal.isAtrImmediateEntryAllowed()
 2142:                 && signal.getTotalScore() >= properties.minimumBuyScore()
 2143:                 && isBullish(signal.getDecision());
 2144:     }
 2145: 
 2146:     /**
 2147:      * FIX-064 / ENA #703, PEPE #756, SUI #802:
 2148:      * Derived BUY/WATCH fields can lag an exhausted or rejected price impulse. Before the three
 2149:      * affected entry routes (SCOUT, DEFERRED_CONTINUATION and ACCUMULATED_EVIDENCE) open fresh
 2150:      * risk, inspect only candles that were fully CLOSED at signal.generatedAt. This deliberately
 2151:      * does not alter market-regime categories, scores, BUY thresholds or accumulated-evidence
 2152:      * semantics. Missing candle/ATR data fails open so data gaps cannot silently disable proven
 2153:      * winning routes. Production and Replay use the same timestamp-bounded repository query.
 2154:      */
 2155:     private ImmediatePriceAction immediatePriceAction(TradeSignal signal) {
 2156:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getAtrAtSignal() == null
 2157:                 || signal.getAtrAtSignal().signum() <= 0) {
 2158:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("raw candle/ATR unavailable; existing behavior preserved");
 2159:         }
 2160: 
 2161:         List<Candle> candles = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesClosedAtOrBefore(
 2162:                 signal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 2163:                 PageRequest.of(0, IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_LOOKBACK_CANDLES));
 2164:         if (candles == null || candles.size() < 2) {
 2165:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("insufficient closed-candle history; existing behavior preserved");
 2166:         }
 2167: 
 2168:         Candle latest = candles.get(0);
 2169:         Candle previous = candles.get(1);
 2170:         if (latest.getOpenPrice() == null || latest.getHighPrice() == null || latest.getLowPrice() == null
 2171:                 || latest.getClosePrice() == null || previous.getClosePrice() == null) {
 2172:             return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive("incomplete closed-candle OHLC; existing behavior preserved");
 2173:         }
 2174: 
 2175:         BigDecimal atr = signal.getAtrAtSignal();
 2176:         BigDecimal range = latest.getHighPrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice()).abs();
 2177:         double closeLocation = range.signum() == 0 ? 0.5d
 2178:                 : latest.getClosePrice().subtract(latest.getLowPrice())
 2179:                         .divide(range, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 2180:         closeLocation = Math.max(0d, Math.min(1d, closeLocation));
 2181: 
 2182:         double bearishBodyAtr = latest.getOpenPrice().subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
 2183:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 2184:         BigDecimal recentHigh = candles.stream()
 2185:                 .map(Candle::getHighPrice)
 2186:                 .filter(Objects::nonNull)
 2187:                 .max(BigDecimal::compareTo)
 2188:                 .orElse(latest.getHighPrice());
 2189:         double recentHighFadeAtr = recentHigh.subtract(latest.getClosePrice())
 2190:                 .max(BigDecimal.ZERO).divide(atr, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP).doubleValue();
 2191: 
 2192:         boolean closeBelowPrevious = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(previous.getClosePrice()) < 0;
 2193:         boolean bearishRejection = latest.getClosePrice().compareTo(latest.getOpenPrice()) < 0
 2194:                 && closeBelowPrevious
 2195:                 && bearishBodyAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_BEARISH_BODY_ATR
 2196:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_BEARISH_REJECTION;
 2197:         boolean exhaustedPop = closeBelowPrevious
 2198:                 && recentHighFadeAtr >= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MIN_RECENT_HIGH_FADE_ATR
 2199:                 && closeLocation <= IMMEDIATE_PRICE_ACTION_MAX_CLOSE_LOCATION_FOR_EXHAUSTED_POP;
 2200: 
 2201:         String summary = "closeLocation=" + format(closeLocation * 100d) + "%"
 2202:                 + ", bearishBody=" + format(bearishBodyAtr) + " ATR"
 2203:                 + ", fadeFromRecentHigh=" + format(recentHighFadeAtr) + " ATR"
 2204:                 + ", closeVsPrevious=" + (closeBelowPrevious ? "LOWER" : "NOT_LOWER");
 2205: 
 2206:         if (bearishRejection || exhaustedPop) {
 2207:             return ImmediatePriceAction.rejected(summary
 2208:                     + (bearishRejection ? ", bearish rejection" : "")
 2209:                     + (exhaustedPop ? ", exhausted-pop fade" : ""));
 2210:         }
 2211:         return ImmediatePriceAction.supportive(summary);
 2212:     }
 2213: 
 2214:     private boolean isSupportiveCurrentSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 2215:         if (isBullish(signal.getDecision()) || isBullish(signal.getOriginalDecision())) return true;
 2216:         return signal.getDecision() == SignalDecision.WATCH
 2217:                 && signal.getTotalScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_SCORE
 2218:                 && signal.getConfidenceScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_CONFIDENCE;
 2219:     }
 2220: 
 2221:     private HardRiskBlock nonAtrHardRiskBlock(TradeSignal signal) {
 2222:         if (!signal.isStrategyEntryAllowed()) {
 2223:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRATEGY_ENTRY_BLOCKED", "Selected market strategy does not allow entry.");
 2224:         }
 2225:         if (!signal.isBtcContextEntryAllowed()) {
 2226:             return HardRiskBlock.block("BTC_CONTEXT_BLOCKED", "BTC context issued an entry veto.");
 2227:         }
 2228:         if (!signal.isDerivativesEntryAllowed()) {
 2229:             return HardRiskBlock.block("DERIVATIVES_BLOCKED", "Funding/open-interest positioning issued an entry veto.");
 2230:         }
 2231:         // With wall-lifecycle intelligence, TARGET_BLOCKED should only remain false-entry-allowed
 2232:         // when the wall is genuinely strong/relevant. Weakening walls no longer reach this hard veto.
 2233:         if (!signal.isLiquidityEntryAllowed()
 2234:                 && signal.getLiquidityStatus() == LiquidityContextStatus.TARGET_BLOCKED) {
 2235:             return HardRiskBlock.block("STRONG_TARGET_WALL",
 2236:                     "A strong nearby target-side wall is still active; execution remains blocked until fresh order-book evidence changes.");
 2237:         }
 2238:         return HardRiskBlock.none();
 2239:     }
 2240: 
 2241:     private Evidence evidence(TradeSignal current) {
 2242:         // FIX-122: initial-entry scope only; additions keep their original evidence.
 2243:         if (stopLossEvidence != null) stopLossEvidence.requireCurrent(current);
 2244:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EVIDENCE_WINDOW);
 2245:         List<TradeSignal> recent = recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt());
 2246: 
 2247:         int buy = 0, watch = 0, neutral = 0, bearish = 0, evidenceScore = 0;
 2248:         int scoreTotal = 0, confidenceTotal = 0, qualityCount = 0;
 2249:         int health = OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START;
 2250:         Instant lastBearishAt = null;
 2251:         Instant oldestObservedAt = null;
 2252:         List<Long> signalIds = new ArrayList<>();
 2253:         List<TradeSignal> observedSequence = new ArrayList<>();
 2254: 
 2255:         for (TradeSignal signal : recent) {
 2256:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt() == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(current.getGeneratedAt())) continue;
 2257:             if (signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff)) break;
 2258:             if (stopLossEvidence != null && !stopLossEvidence.eligible(signal, "EVIDENCE")) continue;
 2259: 
 2260:             if (oldestObservedAt == null || signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(oldestObservedAt)) {
 2261:                 oldestObservedAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
 2262:             }
 2263: 
 2264:             observedSequence.add(signal);
 2265: 
 2266:             SignalDecision decision = signal.getDecision();
 2267:             SignalDecision original = signal.getOriginalDecision();
 2268:             SignalDecision effective = strongerDecision(decision, original);
 2269: 
 2270:             if (effective == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 2271:                 bearish++;
 2272:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_STRONG_SELL, signal);
 2273:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
 2274:                 break; // a strong bearish 1m event is still a true memory boundary
 2275:             }
 2276: 
 2277:             if (effective == SignalDecision.SELL) {
 2278:                 bearish++;
 2279:                 evidenceScore -= WEAK_SELL_EVIDENCE_PENALTY;
 2280:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_SELL, signal);
 2281:                 if (lastBearishAt == null) lastBearishAt = signal.getGeneratedAt();
 2282:                 continue;
 2283:             }
 2284: 
 2285:             if (isBullish(effective)) {
 2286:                 buy++;
 2287:                 evidenceScore += 3;
 2288:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_BUY, signal);
 2289:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
 2290:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
 2291:                 }
 2292:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH
 2293:                     && signal.getTotalScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_SCORE
 2294:                     && signal.getConfidenceScore() >= WATCH_EVIDENCE_MIN_CONFIDENCE) {
 2295:                 watch++;
 2296:                 evidenceScore += 1;
 2297:                 health += scaledHealthContribution(HEALTH_1M_WATCH, signal);
 2298:                 if (signal.getId() != null) {
 2299:                     signalIds.add(signal.getId());
 2300:                 }
 2301:             } else if (decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) {
 2302:                 neutral++;
 2303:                 health += HEALTH_1M_NEUTRAL;
 2304:             }
 2305: 
 2306:             if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || isBullish(decision)) {
 2307:                 scoreTotal += signal.getTotalScore();
 2308:                 confidenceTotal += signal.getConfidenceScore();
 2309:                 qualityCount++;
 2310:             }
 2311:         }
 2312: 
 2313:         int evidenceMomentum = calculateEvidenceMomentum(observedSequence);
 2314:         health += evidenceMomentum;
 2315: 
 2316:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(current, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL, FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE);
 2317:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(current, TREND_INTERVAL, ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE);
 2318: 
 2319:         // Higher timeframe evidence actively restores or weakens opportunity health.
 2320:         // This is deliberately generic: no symbol-specific values are used.
 2321:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(five, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 2322:         health += timeframeHealthContribution(one, TREND_INTERVAL);
 2323: 
 2324:         long ageMinutes = oldestObservedAt == null ? 0
 2325:                 : Math.max(0, Duration.between(oldestObservedAt, current.getGeneratedAt()).toMinutes());
 2326:         // Slow decay: fresh bullish evidence should be able to recover faster than age destroys it.
 2327:         health -= Math.min(6, (int) (ageMinutes / 10));
 2328:         evidenceScore = Math.max(0, evidenceScore);
 2329:         health = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, health));
 2330: 
 2331:         int previousHealth = currentOpportunity(current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
 2332:                 // FIX-122: do not compare fresh health to a pre-stop opportunity snapshot.
 2333:                 .filter(o -> stopLossEvidence == null || stopLossEvidence.eligible(o.getLatestSignal(), "PREVIOUS_HEALTH"))
 2334:                 .map(ExecutionOpportunity::getOpportunityHealth)
 2335:                 .orElse(OPPORTUNITY_HEALTH_START);
 2336:         int healthMomentum = health - previousHealth;
 2337: 
 2338:         return new Evidence(
 2339:                 buy + watch + neutral + bearish,
 2340:                 buy,
 2341:                 watch,
 2342:                 neutral,
 2343:                 bearish,
 2344:                 evidenceScore,
 2345:                 health,
 2346:                 healthMomentum,
 2347:                 evidenceMomentum,
 2348:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) scoreTotal / qualityCount),
 2349:                 qualityCount == 0 ? 0 : Math.round((float) confidenceTotal / qualityCount),
 2350:                 five == null ? null : five.getDecision(),
 2351:                 one == null ? null : one.getDecision(),
 2352:                 lastBearishAt,
 2353:                 signalIds.stream().filter(Objects::nonNull).toList()
 2354:         );
 2355:     }
 2356: 
 2357:     private int calculateEvidenceMomentum(List<TradeSignal> observedDescending) {
 2358:         if (observedDescending == null || observedDescending.size() < 2) return 0;
 2359: 
 2360:         List<TradeSignal> sequence = new ArrayList<>(observedDescending);
 2361:         Collections.reverse(sequence); // oldest -> newest
 2362:         if (sequence.size() > EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_WINDOW) {
 2363:             sequence = new ArrayList<>(sequence.subList(sequence.size() - EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_WINDOW, sequence.size()));
 2364:         }
 2365: 
 2366:         int momentum = 0;
 2367:         for (int i = 1; i < sequence.size(); i++) {
 2368:             TradeSignal previous = sequence.get(i - 1);
 2369:             TradeSignal current = sequence.get(i);
 2370:             int previousStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(previous.getDecision(), previous.getOriginalDecision()));
 2371:             int currentStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(current.getDecision(), current.getOriginalDecision()));
 2372:             int delta = currentStrength - previousStrength;
 2373:             int recencyWeight = Math.min(3, i);
 2374:             momentum += delta * 3 * recencyWeight;
 2375:         }
 2376: 
 2377:         TradeSignal latest = sequence.get(sequence.size() - 1);
 2378:         int latestStrength = decisionStrength(strongerDecision(latest.getDecision(), latest.getOriginalDecision()));
 2379:         momentum += latestStrength * 2;
 2380:         return Math.max(-EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_CAP, Math.min(EVIDENCE_MOMENTUM_CAP, momentum));
 2381:     }
 2382: 
 2383:     private int decisionStrength(SignalDecision decision) {
 2384:         if (decision == null) return 0;
 2385:         return switch (decision) {
 2386:             case STRONG_BUY -> 2;
 2387:             case BUY, WATCH -> 1;
 2388:             case NEUTRAL -> 0;
 2389:             case SELL -> -1;
 2390:             case STRONG_SELL -> -2;
 2391:         };
 2392:     }
 2393: 
 2394:     private int timeframeHealthContribution(TradeSignal signal, String interval) {
 2395:         if (signal == null) return 0;
 2396:         SignalDecision decision = strongerDecision(signal.getDecision(), signal.getOriginalDecision());
 2397:         int base;
 2398:         if (CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL.equals(interval)) {
 2399:             base = switch (decision) {
 2400:                 case BUY, STRONG_BUY -> HEALTH_5M_BUY;
 2401:                 case WATCH -> HEALTH_5M_WATCH;
 2402:                 case SELL -> HEALTH_5M_SELL;
 2403:                 case STRONG_SELL -> HEALTH_5M_STRONG_SELL;
 2404:                 default -> 0;
 2405:             };
 2406:         } else {
 2407:             base = switch (decision) {
 2408:                 case BUY, STRONG_BUY -> HEALTH_1H_BUY;
 2409:                 case WATCH -> HEALTH_1H_WATCH;
 2410:                 case SELL -> HEALTH_1H_SELL;
 2411:                 case STRONG_SELL -> HEALTH_1H_STRONG_SELL;
 2412:                 default -> 0;
 2413:             };
 2414:         }
 2415:         return scaledHealthContribution(base, signal);
 2416:     }
 2417: 
 2418:     private int scaledHealthContribution(int base, TradeSignal signal) {
 2419:         if (base == 0 || signal == null) return 0;
 2420:         int quality = Math.round((signal.getTotalScore() + signal.getConfidenceScore()) / 2.0f);
 2421:         double factor = quality >= 80 ? 1.25 : quality >= 70 ? 1.0 : quality >= 60 ? 0.8 : 0.6;
 2422:         return (int) Math.round(base * factor);
 2423:     }
 2424: 
 2425:     /**
 2426:      * Downstream execution must respect FinalDecisionService as the authority.
 2427:      * originalDecision is retained for audit/explainability only and is used
 2428:      * solely as a null fallback for legacy rows.
 2429:      */
 2430:     private SignalDecision strongerDecision(SignalDecision decision, SignalDecision original) {
 2431:         return decision != null ? decision : original;
 2432:     }
 2433: 
 2434:     private boolean isHardBearishReversal(TradeSignal current, Evidence evidence) {
 2435:         if (current.getDecision() == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) {
 2436:             return true;
 2437:         }
 2438:         return isBearish(evidence.fiveMinute()) || isBearish(evidence.oneHour());
 2439:     }

 2441: 
 2442:     private TradeSignal recentBearishReversalAnchor(TradeSignal current) {
 2443:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return null;
 2444:         Instant cutoff = current.getGeneratedAt().minus(EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_REVERSAL_LOOKBACK);
 2445:         return recentSignals(current.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, current.getGeneratedAt()).stream()
 2446:                 .filter(signal -> signal != null && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null)
 2447:                 .filter(signal -> signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt())
 2448:                         && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(cutoff))
 2449:                 .filter(signal -> isBearish(strongerDecision(signal.getDecision(), signal.getOriginalDecision())))
 2450:                 .filter(signal -> signal.getTotalScore() <= EXCEPTIONAL_PROBE_MAX_PRIOR_SCORE)
 2451:                 .max(java.util.Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
 2452:                 .orElse(null);
 2453:     }
 2454: 
 2455:     private TradeSignal latestAtOrBefore(TradeSignal current, String interval, Duration maxAge) {
 2456:         TradeSignal result = latestSignalAtOrBefore(current.getSymbol(), interval, current.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
 2457:         if (result == null || result.getGeneratedAt() == null
 2458:                 || result.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt().minus(maxAge))) {
 2459:             return null;
 2460:         }
 2461:         return result;
 2462:     }
 2463: 
 2464:     private void updateOpportunityPeak(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity, TradeSignal signal) {
 2465:         if (opportunity == null || signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 2466:         boolean stale = opportunity.getPeakObservedAt() == null
 2467:                 || opportunity.getPeakObservedAt().isBefore(signal.getGeneratedAt().minus(Duration.ofMinutes(15)));
 2468:         boolean stronger = signal.getTotalScore() > opportunity.getPeakScore()
 2469:                 || (signal.getTotalScore() == opportunity.getPeakScore()
 2470:                     && signal.getConfidenceScore() > opportunity.getPeakConfidence());
 2471:         if (!stale && !stronger) return;
 2472: 
 2473:         opportunity.setPeakScore(signal.getTotalScore());
 2474:         opportunity.setPeakConfidence(signal.getConfidenceScore());
 2475:         opportunity.setPeakDecision(signal.getDecision() == null ? null : signal.getDecision().name());
 2476:         opportunity.setPeakRegime(signal.getMarketRegime() == null ? null : signal.getMarketRegime().name());
 2477:         opportunity.setPeakBtcStatus(signal.getBtcContextStatus() == null ? null : signal.getBtcContextStatus().name());
 2478:         opportunity.setPeakLiquidityStatus(signal.getLiquidityStatus() == null ? null : signal.getLiquidityStatus().name());
 2479:         opportunity.setPeakObservedAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
 2480:     }
 2481: 
 2482:     private EvidenceDeterioration assessEvidenceDeterioration(TradeSignal current) {
 2483:         if (current == null || current.getGeneratedAt() == null) return EvidenceDeterioration.none();
 2484:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity = currentOpportunity(
 2485:                 current.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED")).orElse(null);
 2486:         if (opportunity == null || opportunity.getPeakObservedAt() == null) return EvidenceDeterioration.none();
 2487: 
 2488:         // Expired peak memory intentionally stops influencing a rebuilt setup.
 2489:         if (opportunity.getPeakObservedAt().isBefore(current.getGeneratedAt().minus(Duration.ofMinutes(15)))) {
 2490:             return EvidenceDeterioration.none();
 2491:         }
 2492:         // A new equal/stronger setup is recovery, not deterioration. It will replace the peak on save.
 2493:         if (current.getTotalScore() >= opportunity.getPeakScore()
 2494:                 && current.getConfidenceScore() >= opportunity.getPeakConfidence()) {
 2495:             return EvidenceDeterioration.none();
 2496:         }
 2497: 
 2498:         boolean decisionWeakened = decisionRank(current.getDecision()) < decisionRankName(opportunity.getPeakDecision());
 2499:         boolean regimeWeakened = "BREAKOUT".equals(opportunity.getPeakRegime())
 2500:                 && current.getMarketRegime() != com.crypto.domain.MarketRegime.BREAKOUT;
 2501:         boolean btcWeakened = "CONFIRMED".equals(opportunity.getPeakBtcStatus())
 2502:                 && current.getBtcContextStatus() != com.crypto.domain.BtcContextStatus.CONFIRMED;
 2503:         boolean liquidityWeakened = "BULLISH_SUPPORT".equals(opportunity.getPeakLiquidityStatus())
 2504:                 && current.getLiquidityStatus() != LiquidityContextStatus.BULLISH_SUPPORT;
 2505:         int scoreDrop = opportunity.getPeakScore() - current.getTotalScore();
 2506:         int confidenceDrop = opportunity.getPeakConfidence() - current.getConfidenceScore();
 2507:         boolean material = decisionWeakened || regimeWeakened || btcWeakened || liquidityWeakened
 2508:                 || scoreDrop >= 12 || confidenceDrop >= 12;
 2509:         if (!material) return EvidenceDeterioration.none();
 2510: 
 2511:         String explanation = "Accumulated evidence is held because current setup quality materially deteriorated from "
 2512:                 + "the recent peak: peak score/confidence=" + opportunity.getPeakScore() + "/"
 2513:                 + opportunity.getPeakConfidence() + ", current=" + current.getTotalScore() + "/"
 2514:                 + current.getConfidenceScore() + ", decisionWeakened=" + decisionWeakened
 2515:                 + ", regimeWeakened=" + regimeWeakened + ", btcWeakened=" + btcWeakened
 2516:                 + ", liquidityWeakened=" + liquidityWeakened + ". Wait for fresh evidence to rebuild.";
 2517:         return new EvidenceDeterioration(true, explanation);
 2518:     }
 2519: 
 2520:     private int decisionRank(SignalDecision decision) {
 2521:         if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) return 4;
 2522:         if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY) return 3;
 2523:         if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) return 2;
 2524:         if (decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL) return 1;
 2525:         return 0;
 2526:     }
 2527: 
 2528:     private int decisionRankName(String decision) {
 2529:         if (decision == null) return 0;
 2530:         try { return decisionRank(SignalDecision.valueOf(decision)); }
 2531:         catch (IllegalArgumentException ex) { return 0; }
 2532:     }

 2534:     private void saveOpportunity(TradeSignal signal, Evidence evidence, String status, String source,
 2535:                                  int positionPercent, String code, String explanation) {
 2536:         Instant now = Instant.now();
 2537:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity = currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
 2538:                 .orElseGet(() -> ExecutionOpportunity.builder()
 2539:                         .symbol(signal.getSymbol())
 2540:                         .direction("BUY")
 2541:                         .startedAt(signal.getGeneratedAt())
 2542:                         .createdAt(now)
 2543:                         .build());
 2544: 
 2545:         opportunity.setStatus(normalizeStatus(status));
 2546:         opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
 2547:         opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
 2548: 
 2549:         // FIX-055: keep the opportunity's actual price history independent of the
 2550:         // recent-signal lookup window. For a BUY opportunity, the first valid price
 2551:         // is the anchor and the lowest valid observed price is the best entry reference.
 2552:         if (signal.getLatestPrice() != null && signal.getLatestPrice().signum() > 0) {
 2553:             if (opportunity.getAnchorEntryPrice() == null || opportunity.getAnchorEntryPrice().signum() <= 0) {
 2554:                 opportunity.setAnchorEntryPrice(signal.getLatestPrice());
 2555:             }
 2556:             if (opportunity.getBestEntryPrice() == null || opportunity.getBestEntryPrice().signum() <= 0
 2557:                     || signal.getLatestPrice().compareTo(opportunity.getBestEntryPrice()) < 0) {
 2558:                 opportunity.setBestEntryPrice(signal.getLatestPrice());
 2559:             }
 2560:         }
 2561:         // FIX-091 / Fix 8: update or reset the opportunity peak using the current signal.
 2562:         // The peak expires after 15 minutes so an old BUY cannot permanently poison a newly
 2563:         // rebuilt setup. A genuinely stronger current signal establishes a fresh peak.
 2564:         updateOpportunityPeak(opportunity, signal);
 2565: 
 2566:         opportunity.setEvidenceCount(evidence.observationCount());
 2567:         opportunity.setBuyCount(evidence.buyCount());
 2568:         opportunity.setWatchCount(evidence.watchCount());
 2569:         opportunity.setNeutralCount(evidence.neutralCount());
 2570:         opportunity.setBearishCount(evidence.bearishCount());
 2571:         opportunity.setEvidenceScore(evidence.evidenceScore());
 2572:         opportunity.setOpportunityHealth(evidence.opportunityHealth());
 2573:         opportunity.setHealthMomentum(evidence.healthMomentum());
 2574:         opportunity.setEvidenceMomentum(evidence.evidenceMomentum());
 2575:         opportunity.setLastBearishAt(evidence.lastBearishAt());
 2576:         opportunity.setAverageSignalScore(evidence.averageScore());
 2577:         opportunity.setAverageConfidence(evidence.averageConfidence());
 2578:         opportunity.setFiveMinuteDecision(evidence.fiveMinute() == null ? null : evidence.fiveMinute().name());
 2579:         opportunity.setOneHourDecision(evidence.oneHour() == null ? null : evidence.oneHour().name());
 2580:         opportunity.setExecutionSource(source);
 2581:         opportunity.setRecommendedPositionPercent(positionPercent);
 2582:         opportunity.setDecisionCode(code);
 2583:         opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(explanation);
 2584:         opportunity.setUpdatedAt(now);
 2585:         saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
 2586:     }
 2587: 
 2588:     private void closeOpportunity(TradeSignal signal, String status, String code, String explanation) {
 2589:         currentOpportunity(signal.getSymbol(), List.of("BUILDING", "WEAKENING", "BLOCKED", "CONFIRMED"))
 2590:                 .ifPresent(opportunity -> {
 2591:                     opportunity.setStatus(status);
 2592:                     opportunity.setLatestSignal(signal);
 2593:                     opportunity.setLastEvidenceAt(signal.getGeneratedAt());
 2594:                     opportunity.setDecisionCode(code);
 2595:                     opportunity.setDecisionExplanation(explanation);
 2596:                     opportunity.setUpdatedAt(Instant.now());
 2597:                     saveOpportunityEntity(opportunity);
 2598:                 });
 2599:     }

## FINDINGS

**PROJECT EVIDENCE — frozen cohort confirmed.** DBQ-FTS-005 identifies exactly these six `crypto_ai.paper_position` rows, all `CLOSED`, negative P&L, and closed timestamps present. I did not substitute `STOPPED` rows or Replay data.

| Position | Symbol | Open UTC → KSA | Close UTC → KSA | Hold | P&L | Recorded close reason |
|---:|---|---|---|---:|---:|---|
| 182 | ACEUSDT | 2026-10-12 09:57:06.476246 → 12:57:06.476246 | 10:05:20.486035 → 13:05:20.486035 | 8m 14.010s | -0.031847133758 | `SELL_CONFIRMED` |
| 172 | SHIBUSDT | 06:36:05.087117 → 09:36:05.087117 | 07:15:45.484258 → 10:15:45.484258 | 39m 40.397s | -0.045537340619 | `HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED` |
| 162 | DOGEUSDT | 04:02:06.414818 → 07:02:06.414818 | 04:26:04.116278 → 07:26:04.116278 | 23m 57.701s | -0.051694428489 | `STRONG_SELL` |
| 158 | XRPUSDT | 02:35:04.811478 → 05:35:04.811478 | 03:30:06.638124 → 06:30:06.638124 | 55m 01.827s | -0.031896796144 | `SELL_CONFIRMED` |
| 143 | EGLDUSDT | 2026-10-11 22:40:04.857700 → 2026-10-12 01:40:04.857700 | 22:43:36.229162 → 01:43:36.229162 | 3m 31.371s | -0.037193156459 | `PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT` |
| 137 | EGLDUSDT | 20:55:04.510238 → 23:55:04.510238 | 21:00:12.121700 → 2026-10-12 00:00:12.121700 | 5m 07.611s | -0.075018754689 | `SELL_CONFIRMED` |

Total realized loss: **-0.273187610158**.

**PROJECT EVIDENCE — cohort scope caveat.** There are 75 `STOPPED` rows and all have negative P&L (DBQ-FTS-002), but the mandatory frozen selection expressly filters `status='CLOSED'`. Thus this is not a claim about the six most recent losing positions across every terminal status; it is exactly the mandated CLOSED-loss cohort.

**PROJECT EVIDENCE — exit-label distribution.** Five of six positions carry an adverse signal/protection-oriented close label: three `SELL_CONFIRMED`, one `HTF_SELL_CONFIRMED`, and one `STRONG_SELL`. One carries `PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT`. This supports prioritising exit-decision and protection evidence in subsequent iterations; it does not yet establish that any exit was correct, timely, or defective.

**PROJECT EVIDENCE — source behaviour.** `PaperTradingService` orders mechanical exits ahead of normal bearish-signal processing: target, profit lock, stop loss, hard profit floor, then validated `SELL`/`STRONG_SELL`. `paper_position` persists both entry and exit rationale plus entry/exit signal links. The present evidence lacks those fields, so no label can yet be translated into a proven triggering rule.

**AGENT INFERENCE.** The short-loss positions 143 and 137 (both EGLDUSDT) deserve comparison for repeated-entry/exit conditions, but repeat symbol alone is not evidence of a defect or duplicate entry.

## EVIDENCE

- DBQ-FTS-001: actual `paper_position` schema.
- DBQ-FTS-002 through DBQ-FTS-005: frozen cohort selection and row facts.
- `PaperPosition.java`: durable fields for entry/exit prices, reasons, and signal links.
- `PaperTradingService.java`: current code order for position-close evaluation.
- No external research used.

## UNKNOWNS

- Full position economics: quantity, entry/exit price, stop, target, and narrative fields.
- Entry and exit signal IDs/decisions, timestamps, interval, data quality, and contemporaneous context.
- Wallet execution records, fees, partial executions, and P&L reconciliation.
- Accepted price-event/protection evidence, including whether a label reflects a first threshold breach.
- Signal-processing timing, candle lineage/data quality, logs, configuration/version active at the time, and Replay comparability.
- The requested Codex iteration-1 private artifacts and `db/CODEX/` contained no files in the permitted paths, so they add no evidence for this iteration.

### DB_REQUEST
QueryId: DBQ-CODEX-I2-001  
Purpose: Retrieve the complete authoritative `paper_position` evidence for the fixed six-position cohort, including entry/exit linkage and rationale, without widening the cohort.  
SQL:
```sql
SELECT
    id,
    symbol,
    side,
    status,
    quantity,
    entry_price,
    stop_loss,
    take_profit,
    exit_price,
    realized_pnl,
    close_reason,
    entry_reason,
    exit_reason,
    signal_id,
    exit_signal_id,
    opened_at,
    closed_at
FROM paper_position
WHERE id IN (182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137)
ORDER BY FIELD(id, 182, 172, 162, 158, 143, 137);
```
### END_DB_REQUEST

### DB_REQUEST

DESCRIBE trade_signal;
